4      *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5      *&      Module  STATUS_0100  OUTPUT
6      *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
7      *       Set Status of screen 100.
8      *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
9      MODULE status_0100 OUTPUT.
10       PERFORM   append_isttab_100.
11       SET PF-STATUS 'S100' EXCLUDING ist_tab.
12       SET TITLEBAR 'T100_01'.
13       IF sy-ucomm = 'BACK'.
14         PERFORM release_lock.
15       ENDIF.
16     ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_0100  OUTPUT
17     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
18     *&      Module  set_screen_100  OUTPUT
19     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
20     *       Set Screen element Attributes of Screen 100.
21     *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
22     MODULE set_screen_100 OUTPUT.
23
24     *&--> disable document field.
25       LOOP AT SCREEN.
26         IF screen-group1 = '001'.
27           screen-active  = '0' .
28           MODIFY SCREEN.
29         ENDIF.
30       ENDLOOP.
31       IF ok_code = 'CHAN' OR ok_code = 'DISP' OR ok_code   = 'DELE' OR
32          ok_code = 'REST' OR ok_code = 'BGLC' OR ok_code   = 'AMEND'.
33         LOOP AT SCREEN.
34           IF screen-group1    = '001'.
35             screen-invisible = '0'  .
36             screen-active    = '1'    .
37             screen-input     = '1'.
38             MODIFY SCREEN.
39           ENDIF.
40         ENDLOOP.
41       ENDIF.
42     *&--<
43     ENDMODULE.                 " set_screen_100  OUTPUT
44     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
45     *&      Module  set_screen_0105  OUTPUT
46     *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
47     *      Set Screen element in Screen - 0105.
48     *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
49     MODULE set_screen_0105 OUTPUT.
50
51     *  IF zmm_emdhdr-trans NE 'EMD'.
52     *    LOOP AT SCREEN.
53     *      IF SCREEN-GROUP3 = 'EMD'.
54     *        SCREEN-INPUT  = 0.
55     *        SCREEN-INVISIBLE  = 1.
56     *        MODIFY SCREEN.
57     *      ENDIF.
58     *    ENDLOOP.
59     *  ENDIF.
60
61     *&--> Make  Table Control invisible in screen 105 IF Header data not *&
62     * entered.
63
64       IF g_okhdr = 'X' OR g_tfxm = 'X'.
65         tc_105-invisible = 0.
66         LOOP AT SCREEN.
67           IF screen-name = 'ITEMDTLS' OR screen-name = 'INSERT' OR
68              screen-name = 'DELET' OR screen-name = 'COPY' OR
69              screen-name = 'PASTE'.
70             IF screen-group1 = 'I01'.
71               screen-active = 0.
72               MODIFY SCREEN.
73             ENDIF.
74           ENDIF.
75         ENDLOOP.
76       ELSE.
77         CLEAR tc_105-invisible .
78
79         LOOP AT SCREEN.
80           IF screen-name = 'ITEMDTLS'.
81             IF screen-group1 = 'I01'.
82               screen-active = 1.
83               MODIFY SCREEN.
84             ENDIF.
85           ENDIF.
86         ENDLOOP.
87       ENDIF.
88
89     *&----------------------------------------------------------&*
90     * SRM Changes +002
91     *&----------------------------------------------------------&*
92
93       IF sy-ucomm = 'TFEXM' AND g_okhdr NE 'X' AND g_tfxm NE 'X'.
94         CLEAR tc_105-invisible .
95         LOOP AT SCREEN.
96           IF screen-name = 'ITEMDTLS'.
97             IF screen-group1 = 'I01'.
98               screen-active = 1.
99               MODIFY SCREEN.
100            ENDIF.
101          ENDIF.
102        ENDLOOP.
103      ENDIF.
104
105    *&-- End of Change
106    *&----------------------------------------------------------&*
107
108
109    *&--< Make Input Field disable When Action is Display or
110    *   Delete or Reset
111    *&-->
112      IF prev_okcode = 'DISP' OR g_reset = 1 OR
113        prev_okcode  = 'DELE' OR ok_code = 'DELT'
114        OR prev_okcode = 'REST'.
115        CLEAR tc_105-invisible .
116
117        LOOP AT tc_105-cols INTO tc_col.
118          IF tc_col-screen-group2 = 'G02' OR tc_col-screen-group2 = 'C02'.
119            tc_col-screen-input = 0.
120            MODIFY tc_105-cols FROM tc_col.
121          ENDIF.
122        ENDLOOP.
123      ENDIF.
124    *&--<
125      IF  g_reset = '1'.                                        "27.01.04
126        CLEAR tc_105-invisible .
127        LOOP AT tc_105-cols INTO tc_col.
128          IF tc_col-screen-group2 = 'G02'.
129            tc_col-screen-input = 0.
130            MODIFY tc_105-cols FROM tc_col.
131          ENDIF.
132        ENDLOOP.
133
134        LOOP AT SCREEN.
135          IF screen-name = 'ZMM_EMDHDR-CURRENCY'.
136            CLEAR screen-input .
137            CLEAR screen-active .
138            MODIFY SCREEN.
139          ENDIF.
140        ENDLOOP.
141      ENDIF.
142
143    *&--<
144    *&-->  Disable input fields if trans is initial.
145
146      LOOP AT SCREEN.
147        IF  zmm_emdhdr-trans IS INITIAL .
148          IF screen-group2 = 'G02'.
149            screen-input  = 0.
150            SCREEN-INVISIBLE  = 1.
151            MODIFY SCREEN.
152          ENDIF.
153        ELSE .
154          IF screen-group2 = 'G02'.
155            screen-input  = 1.
156            MODIFY SCREEN.
157          ENDIF.
158        ENDIF.
159      ENDLOOP.
160    *&--<
161
162    *#--< Adding Ref.w.rfq and vendor radio buttons   Added on 01.02.2023 by hrishi n
163    IF zmm_emdhdr-trans EQ 'EMD'.
164
165     IF g_action = 'Create'.
166        IF REF_VEN NE 'X'.
167           REF_RFQ = 'X'.
168           Clear : REF_VEN,ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO,G_VNAME1.
169        ELSEIF REF_VEN = 'X'.
170           Clear REF_RFQ.
171        ENDIF.
172      ELSEIF g_action = 'Change' OR g_action = 'Display'.
173        IF ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO IS NOT INITIAL.
174          REF_VEN = 'X'.
175          Clear : REF_RFQ,
176          ZMM_EMDHDR-ebeln.
177        ELSE.
178          REF_RFQ = 'X'.
179          Clear REF_VEN.
180        ENDIF.
181      ENDIF.
182
183      IF REF_RFQ = 'X'.
184        LOOP AT SCREEN.
185          IF SCREEN-GROUP4 = 'VEN'.
186            SCREEN-INPUT  = 0.
187            SCREEN-INVISIBLE  = 1.
188            MODIFY SCREEN.
189          ENDIF.
190        ENDLOOP.
191      ELSEIF REF_VEN = 'X'.
192        LOOP AT SCREEN.
193          IF SCREEN-GROUP4 = 'RFQ'.
194            SCREEN-INPUT  = 0.

196            MODIFY SCREEN.
197          ENDIF.
198
199          IF SCREEN-NAME = 'ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO'.
200            SCREEN-REQUIRED = '1'.
201            MODIFY SCREEN.
202          ENDIF.
203        ENDLOOP.
204      ENDIF.
205
206      IF ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN IS INITIAL AND ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO IS INITIAL.
207        LOOP AT SCREEN.
208         IF SCREEN-GROUP4 = 'G04'.
209            SCREEN-INPUT  = 0.
210            SCREEN-INVISIBLE  = 1.
211            MODIFY SCREEN.
212         ENDIF.

450    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
451    *&      Module  STATUS_0105  OUTPUT
452    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
453    *     PF Status of Screen 0105
454    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
455    MODULE status_0105 OUTPUT.
456      PERFORM modify_isttab.
457      SET PF-STATUS 'S105' EXCLUDING ist_tab .
458      IF prev_okcode = 'CREA'.
459        IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
460          SET TITLEBAR 'T105_01' WITH g_action  text-001  .
461        ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD'.
462          SET TITLEBAR 'T105_01' WITH  g_action text-002   .
463        ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
464          SET TITLEBAR 'T105_01' WITH  g_action text-003  .
465        ENDIF.
466      ELSE.
467        IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
468          SET TITLEBAR 'T105' WITH g_action  text-001 text-010 g_idocno.
469        ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD'.
470          SET TITLEBAR 'T105' WITH  g_action text-002 text-010 g_idocno .
471        ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
472          SET TITLEBAR 'T105' WITH  g_action text-003 text-010 g_idocno.
473        ENDIF.
474      ENDIF.
475    ENDMODULE.                 " STATUS_0105  OUTPUT
476    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
477    *&      Module  tc105_move_data  OUTPUT
478    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
479    *      Move Data from Work area to Table Control
480    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
481    MODULE tc105_move_data OUTPUT.
482      wa_emddtl-currency = zmm_emdhdr-currency .
483      PERFORM get_item_status USING wa_emddtl-status .
484      MOVE g_status TO  wa_emddtl-stat_desc.
485      MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emddtl TO wa_tc105 .
486      CLEAR g_status.
487      LOOP AT SCREEN.
488    *&------> If the document status is N then only activate entire row
489        IF prev_okcode = 'CHAN'  .
490          IF  wa_emddtl-status NE 'N' AND  wa_emddtl-status NE space.
491            IF screen-name NE 'WA_TC105-SEL' .
492              screen-input = 0.
493            ELSE.
494              screen-input = 1.
495            ENDIF.
496          ELSE.
497            screen-input = 1.
498          ENDIF.
499          IF    screen-name = 'WA_TC105-SEL' AND
500              wa_tc105-status NE 'N'.
501            screen-active = 0.
502          ELSE.
503            screen-active = 1.
504          ENDIF.
505          MODIFY SCREEN.
506        ENDIF.
507    *&------<
508        IF screen-name = 'WA_TC105-STATUS'.
509          screen-invisible = 1.
510          MODIFY SCREEN.
511        ELSE.
512          screen-invisible = 0.
513          MODIFY SCREEN.
514        ENDIF.
515      ENDLOOP.
516    *-------- Disable field BANK,BRANCH,ADDR1,ADDR2 when instrument is IV.
517      LOOP AT SCREEN.
518        IF wa_tc105-inst_type = 'IV'.
519          IF screen-group1 = 'IV1'  .
520            screen-input = 0.
521            MODIFY SCREEN.
522          ENDIF.
523        ENDIF.
524      ENDLOOP.
525    * Start of addition by Saravanan M on 08/04/2009
526      IF l_result = '1' AND prev_okcode = 'ECREA'.
527        zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
528      ELSEIF l_result = '3' AND prev_okcode = 'ECREA'.
529        zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD'.
530      ENDIF.
531    * End of addition by Saravanan M on 08/04/2009
532    ENDMODULE.                 " tc105_move_data  OUTPUT

889      DATA g_header_text TYPE string.

902        WHEN 'F'.

904        WHEN 'G'.
905          g_header_text = 'Request for BG/LC Return'.
906        WHEN 'H'.
907          g_header_text = 'Request for BG/LC Invoke'.
908        WHEN 'I'.
909          g_header_text = 'Document parked in FI'.
910        WHEN 'J'.
911          g_header_text = 'Document completed'.
912        WHEN 'K'.
913          g_header_text = 'Partially  Parked'.
914        WHEN 'L'.
915          g_header_text = 'Partial Refund'.
916        WHEN 'M'.
917          g_header_text = 'Partial Forfeit'.
918        WHEN 'N'.
919          g_header_text = 'Receipt/Deposit'.
920        WHEN 'O'.

922        WHEN 'P'.
923          g_header_text = 'Accepted by FI BG/LC'.
924        WHEN 'Q'.
925          g_header_text = 'Document in process'.
926        WHEN 'R'.
927          g_header_text = 'Refund'.
928        WHEN 'S'.
929          g_header_text = 'Submitted to FI (BG/LC)'.
930        WHEN 'T'.
931          g_header_text = 'Fully Forfeit'.
932        WHEN 'U'.
933          g_header_text = 'Fully Parked'.
934        WHEN 'V'.
935          g_header_text = 'Invoke'.
936        WHEN 'W'.
937          g_header_text = 'Request for BG/LC  Return & Invoke'.
938        WHEN 'X'.

940        WHEN 'Y'.
941          g_header_text = 'Deny by Bank'.
942        WHEN 'Z'.

958      ELSEIF g_doccat = text-016.

964    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
965    *&      Module  set_screen_0110  OUTPUT
966    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
967    *       Set Screen - 0110.
968    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
969    MODULE set_screen_0110 OUTPUT.
970    *&-----> iF INPUT is valid  Document the set amount field Active
971      IF zmm_emdhdr-docno IS INITIAL AND g_doccat IS INITIAL   .
972        LOOP AT SCREEN.
973          IF screen-group1 = 'G01' OR  screen-group2 = 'G02' OR
974             screen-group1 = 'D01'.
975            screen-invisible   = 1.
976            screen-active     = 0.
977            MODIFY SCREEN.
978          ENDIF.
979        ENDLOOP.
980      ELSE.
981        LOOP AT SCREEN.
982          IF screen-group1 = 'G01' OR  screen-group2 = 'G02' OR
983             screen-group1 = 'D01'.
984            screen-invisible   = 0.
985            screen-active     = 1.
986            MODIFY SCREEN.
987          ENDIF.
988        ENDLOOP.
989      ENDIF.
990      IF ok_code NE 'CRET'.
991        LOOP AT SCREEN.
992          IF screen-group1 = 'D01'.
993            screen-invisible = 1.
994            screen-active   = 0 .
995            MODIFY SCREEN.
996          ENDIF.
997        ENDLOOP.
998      ELSE.
999        LOOP AT SCREEN.
1000         IF screen-group1 = 'D01'.
1001           screen-invisible = 0.
1002           screen-active   = 1 .
1003           screen-input    = 1.
1004           MODIFY SCREEN.
1005         ENDIF.
1006       ENDLOOP.
1007     ENDIF.
1008     IF g_ref =  1.
1009       LOOP AT SCREEN.
1010         IF screen-group1 = 'G01'.
1011           screen-invisible = 1.
1012           screen-active   = 0 .
1013           MODIFY SCREEN.
1014         ENDIF.
1015       ENDLOOP.
1016     ENDIF.
1017   *&-------<
1018     IF g_ref = 0 AND g_doccat = text-018.
1019       LOOP AT SCREEN.
1020         IF screen-group2 = 'G02'.
1021           screen-invisible = 0.
1022           screen-active   = 1 .
1023           screen-input    = 1.
1024           MODIFY SCREEN.
1025         ENDIF.
1026       ENDLOOP.
1027     ELSE.
1028       LOOP AT SCREEN.
1029         IF screen-group2 = 'G02'.
1030           screen-invisible = 1.
1031           screen-active   = 0 .
1032           screen-input   = 0.
1033           MODIFY SCREEN.
1034         ENDIF.
1035       ENDLOOP.
1036     ENDIF.
1037   *&-------<
1038
1039   * Start of Addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 18/06/2009
1040     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS' AND sy-dynnr = '0110' AND zmm_emdref-rscode = 120.
1041       LOOP AT SCREEN.
1042         IF screen-name = 'ZMM_EMDREF-AMOUNT'.
1043           screen-active   = 1.
1044           screen-input   = 0.
1045           MODIFY SCREEN.
1046         ENDIF.
1047       ENDLOOP.
1048     ENDIF.
1049   * End of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 18/06/2009
1050   ENDMODULE.                 " set_screen_0110  OUTPUT

562          ENDIF.
563        WHEN  'EXIT' .
564          IF G_REF = 0.
565            PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING TEXT-005 TEXT-004 .
566            IF G_ANS = 'J'.
567              PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
568              LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100'.
569            ENDIF.
570          ELSE.
571            PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
572            LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100'.
573          ENDIF.
574      ENDCASE.
575    ENDMODULE.                 " exit_command_0110  INPUT
576    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
577    *&      Module  user_command_0110  INPUT
578    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
579    *       User Commands in Screen - 0110
580    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
581    MODULE USER_COMMAND_0110 INPUT.
582      DATA: L_NR LIKE INRI-NRRANGENR.
583      CLEAR WA_EMDHDR_T02 .
584      DATA: LV_CODE LIKE SY-UCOMM.
585      OK_CODE = SY-UCOMM.
586    * Begin of <RD1K963111>
587      IF NOT SY-UCOMM IS INITIAL.
588        LV_CODE = SY-UCOMM.
589      ENDIF.
590
591      IF G_DOCCAT = 'REFUND' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS'.
592        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '120'.
593      ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = 'REFUND' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'.
594        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '220'.
595      ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = 'REFUND' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'SDT'.
596        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '350'.
597      ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = 'FORFEIT' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'.
598        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '230'.
599      ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = 'FORFEIT' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'SDT'.
600        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '360'.
601      ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = 'EMD-SD CNV' AND ( LV_CODE = 'CRET' OR LV_CODE = 'CHNG' ) AND G_BALAMT IS NOT INITIAL AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'.
602        ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '210'.
603      ENDIF.
604
605      IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' AND ZMM_EMDREF-RSCODE = '120' AND SY-DYNNR = '0110'.
606        G_BALAMT = '0.00'.
607      ENDIF.
608
609    * End of <RD1K963111>
610      IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO IS INITIAL AND G_REF = 1.         "07.02.04
611        PERFORM CHECK_DOCNO.
612        PERFORM GET_VENDOR_NAME.
613      ENDIF.
614      IF G_REF = 0.
615      ENDIF.
616      CASE OK_CODE.
617        WHEN 'SAVE'.
618          CLEAR L_NR.
619          IF G_DOCCAT   = TEXT-015.   "Refund
620            L_NR = '02'.
621            MOVE:  'R' TO G_DOCSTAT  ,
622                   'R' TO ZMM_EMDREF-RFCSTAT.
623
624            G_TITEL     = TEXT-015 .
625            G_TEXT      = TEXT-012 .
626          ELSEIF G_DOCCAT = TEXT-016. "Forfeit
627            L_NR = '03'.
628            MOVE: 'F'  TO  G_DOCSTAT  ,
629                  'F'  TO ZMM_EMDREF-RFCSTAT.
630
631            G_TITEL     = TEXT-016.
632            G_TEXT      = TEXT-013 .
633          ELSEIF G_DOCCAT  = TEXT-018. "EMD-SD Conversion
634            L_NR = '04'.
635            MOVE : 'C' TO  G_DOCSTAT  ,
636                   'C' TO  ZMM_EMDREF-RFCSTAT.
637
638            ZMM_EMDREF-TRANS = 'SDT'.
639            G_TITEL     = TEXT-017.
640            G_TEXT      = TEXT-014 .
641          ENDIF.
642          PERFORM POPUP_CONF_110 USING G_TITEL G_TEXT.
643          IF G_RFC = 'J'.
644            CLEAR G_OKHDR .
645            PERFORM GET_RFC_DOCNO USING L_NR.
646            PERFORM SAVE_DATA_110 ON COMMIT.
647            IF G_SAVE_H = 0 AND G_SAVE_I = 0.
648              COMMIT WORK.
649              MESSAGE S428(ZMM) WITH G_TITEL G_DOCNO  .
650              PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
651              CLEAR G_DOCNO.
652            ENDIF.
653            LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100'.
654          ENDIF.
655        WHEN 'CHNG' OR 'DISP' OR 'DELE' OR 'UNDEL'.
656          PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
657          PREV_OKCODE = OK_CODE .
658          G_RFC_CHK = 1.
659          CALL SCREEN '0150'.
660        WHEN 'HEAD' .
661          MOVE  WA_EMDHDR_T02-DOCNO  TO  WA_EMDHDR-DOCNO.
662          MOVE  WA_EMDHDR_T02-CRON   TO  WA_EMDHDR-CRON .
663          MOVE  WA_EMDHDR_T02-TRANS  TO  WA_EMDHDR-TRANS .
664          PERFORM DISP_HEAD USING WA_EMDHDR-DOCNO
665                                  WA_EMDHDR-CRON
666                                  WA_EMDHDR-TRANS.
667        WHEN 'SUBFI'.
668          CALL TRANSACTION 'ZMMRFCSUB'.
669        WHEN 'RFCR'.
670          CALL TRANSACTION 'ZMMRFC' .
671      ENDCASE.
672    ENDMODULE.                 " user_command_0110  INPUT
673    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
674    *&      Module  exit_command_0115  INPUT
675    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
676    *     Exit Command - screen 115
677    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
678    MODULE EXIT_COMMAND_0115 INPUT.
679      CLEAR G_ANS.
680      OK_CODE = SY-UCOMM.
681      REFRESH IST_EMDREF_01.
682      REFRESH CONTROL 'TC_120' FROM SCREEN '0120'.
683      TS_115-ACTIVETAB = 'RFCHD' .
684      G_DYNNR = '0130'.
685      CASE OK_CODE.

687          IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CRET' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHNG' OR
688            PREV_OKCODE  = 'UNDEL'.

690              PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING TEXT-005 TEXT-004 .
691              IF G_ANS = 'J'.
692                PERFORM RELEASE_LOCK_RFC USING ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO .
693                PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
694                LEAVE TO SCREEN '0110'.
695              ENDIF.
696            ELSE.
697              PERFORM RELEASE_LOCK_RFC USING ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO .
698              PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
699              LEAVE TO SCREEN '0110'.
700            ENDIF.
701          ELSE.
702            PERFORM RELEASE_LOCK_REF .
703            PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
704            LEAVE TO  SCREEN '0110'.
705          ENDIF.
706      ENDCASE.
707    ENDMODULE.                 " exit_command_0115  INPUT
708    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
709    *&      Module  user_command_0115  INPUT
710    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
711    *       User Commands Screen - 0115
712    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
713    MODULE USER_COMMAND_0115 INPUT.
714      IF  ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO IS INITIAL.
715        REFRESH CONTROL 'TC_120' FROM SCREEN '0120'.
716      ENDIF.
717      OK_CODE = SY-UCOMM .
718      CASE OK_CODE .
719        WHEN 'SAVE'.
720          PERFORM SAVE_CHANGE_POPUP USING TEXT-006.
721          IF G_SC_ANS = '1'.
722            PERFORM SAVE_DATA_115 ON COMMIT.
723            IF  G_SAVE_I = 0.
724              COMMIT WORK.
725              MESSAGE I412(ZMM).
726              PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_115.
727              LEAVE TO SCREEN '0110'.
728            ELSE.
729              ROLLBACK WORK.
730            ENDIF.
731          ENDIF.
732        WHEN 'DELE'.
733          PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING TEXT-022 TEXT-023 .
734          IF G_ANS = 'J'.
735            PERFORM DELETE_RFC_DOC.
736            PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_115.
737            LEAVE TO SCREEN '0110'.
738          ENDIF.
739    *----------- Reset RFC Document
740        WHEN 'RESET'.
741          PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING TEXT-051 TEXT-053 .
742          IF G_ANS = 'J'.
743            PERFORM REST_RFC_DOC USING ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO.
744            PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_115.
745            LEAVE TO SCREEN '0110'.
746          ENDIF.
747        WHEN 'HEAD' .
748          PERFORM GET_HEADER.
749        WHEN  'RFCHD'.
750          TS_115-ACTIVETAB = OK_CODE .
751          G_DYNNR = '0130'.
752        WHEN  'RFC'.
753          TS_115-ACTIVETAB = OK_CODE .
754          G_DYNNR = '0120'.
755    *&---> Get Refund/Forfeit/EMD-SD Converted Data
756          PERFORM GET_RFC_DATA.
757    *&---<
758        WHEN 'ME43'.
759          SET PARAMETER ID 'ANF' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN.
760          CALL TRANSACTION 'ME43' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
761          CLEAR OK_CODE .
762    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
763    *    WHEN 'ME23'.
764         WHEN 'ME23N'.
765    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
766    *+004
767          CLEAR G_BSTYP .
768          PERFORM GET_BSTYP.
769          IF G_BSTYP = 'F'.
770            SET PARAMETER ID 'BES' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
771            CALL TRANSACTION 'ME23N' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
772          ELSEIF G_BSTYP = 'K'.
773            SET PARAMETER ID 'CTR' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
774            CALL TRANSACTION 'ME33K' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
775          ENDIF.
776          CLEAR OK_CODE .
777        WHEN  'ENMT' .
778          IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' OR
779             ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'.
780            SET PARAMETER ID 'ANF' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN.
781            CALL TRANSACTION 'ME43' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
782            CLEAR OK_CODE .
783          ELSE.
784            CLEAR G_BSTYP .
785            PERFORM GET_BSTYP.
786            IF G_BSTYP = 'F'.
787              SET PARAMETER ID 'BES' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
788              CALL TRANSACTION 'ME23N' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
789            ELSEIF G_BSTYP = 'K'.
790              SET PARAMETER ID 'CTR' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
791              CALL TRANSACTION 'ME33K' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
792            ENDIF.
793            CLEAR OK_CODE .
794          ENDIF.
795      ENDCASE.
796    ENDMODULE.                 " user_command_0115  INPUT

858    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
859    *&      Module  check_header  INPUT
860    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
861    *       Check Header Data  of screen 105
862    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
863    MODULE CHECK_HEADER INPUT.
864
865      IF   ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS    IS INITIAL    OR
866    *       zmm_emdhdr-ebeln    IS INITIAL    OR
867           ZMM_EMDHDR-CURRENCY IS INITIAL    OR
868           ZMM_EMDHDR-CO_CODE  IS INITIAL .
869        G_OKHDR = 'X'.
870      ELSE.
871        CLEAR G_OKHDR .
872      ENDIF.
873
874    *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
875    * SRM Changes +002  29.09.2004
876      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'ECREA' OR G_TTYPE = 'SRM'.
877        IF   ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS    IS INITIAL    OR
878             ZMM_EMDHDR-CURRENCY IS INITIAL    OR
879             ZMM_EMDHDR-CO_CODE  IS INITIAL    OR
880             ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO IS INITIAL    OR
881             ZMM_EMDHDR-EBIDNO   IS INITIAL.
882          G_OKHDR = 'X'.
883        ELSE.
884          CLEAR G_OKHDR .
885        ENDIF.
886    ** For SRM Bid Only Trasaction type TFS and EMD is Allowed.
887    ** Otherwise raise error message
888        IF NOT ( ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' OR
889                 ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS =  'EMD' ).
890          MESSAGE E507(ZMM).
891        ENDIF.
892      ENDIF.
893    *&--->
894      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'ECREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN'.
895        IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO IS INITIAL.
896          PERFORM CHECK_VENDOR USING ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO .
897        ENDIF.
898        IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-EBIDNO IS INITIAL.
899          PERFORM CHECK_EBIDNO .
900        ENDIF.
901      ENDIF.
902    *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
903
904    *&------> Check INR incase of intrument 'IP'.
905      IF WA_TC105-INST_TYPE = 'IP' AND
906        ZMM_EMDHDR-CURRENCY NE 'INR'.
907        MESSAGE E554(ZMM).
908      ENDIF.
909    * Begin of <RD1K963111>
910    * To validate the Vendor & E-Bid No passing to SRM system
911      IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' AND ( PREV_OKCODE = 'ECREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN' ).
912        IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-VENDORNO IS INITIAL AND NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-EBIDNO IS INITIAL.
913          PERFORM CHECK_EBID_VEDNOR.
914        ENDIF.
915      ENDIF.
916    * Begin of <RD1K963111>
917    *&------<
918    *&--> Check Document no When Create Mode
919      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN'.
920        PERFORM CHECK_EBELN.
921      ENDIF.
922    *&--<
923    *&--> Get Vendor code and Tender no
924      PERFORM GET_VENDORNO_TENDERNO.
925    *&--<
926    *&--> Check Location
927      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN'
928         OR PREV_OKCODE = 'ECREA'.
929        IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS'.
930          IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-PLACE IS INITIAL.
931            PERFORM CHECK_LOC.
932          ENDIF.
933        ENDIF.
934      ENDIF.
935    *&--<
936    *&--> Check  TFS doc. same RFQ and Currency Exist or not.
937      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CREA' AND WA_TC105-INST_TYPE IS INITIAL.
938        IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' OR ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'
939           OR  ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'SDT'.
940          PERFORM CHECK_RFQ .
941        ENDIF.
942      ENDIF.
943    *&--<
944    *&----->  Check Company code.
945      IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-CO_CODE IS INITIAL.
946        PERFORM CHECK_COMPANY_CODE .
947      ENDIF.
948    *------<
949    *&---> Check whether Vendor code exist in Entered Company code or not
950      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN'.
951        IF NOT G_VCODE IS INITIAL AND NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-CO_CODE IS INITIAL.
952          PERFORM CHECK_COMP_CODE USING G_VCODE ZMM_EMDHDR-CO_CODE .
953        ENDIF.
954      ENDIF.
955    *&-----<
956    ENDMODULE.                 " check_header  INPUT
957    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
958    *&      Module  check_docno  INPUT
959    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
960    *       Check Docuement no
961    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
962    MODULE CHECK_DOCNO INPUT.
963
964    *-----------------------------------------------------------------*
965    ** Check Document *---* If Document is Converted from EMD TO SD
966    *  Don't allow to  Edit.

972      ENDIF.

977    *  Don't allow for Delete                                       *
978    *----------------------------------------------------------------*

980        PERFORM  CHECK_EMDCONV_DOC USING ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO.
981      ENDIF.
982    *----------------------------------------------------------------*
983    *------- Check Document Status *---------------------------------*
984      PERFORM CHECK_RFC_DOCU_STATUS.
985    *-----------------------------------------------------------------*
986    *------   Check Balace amount when 'RESET'    --------------------*
987      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'UNDEL'.
988        PERFORM CHECK_AMOUNT_BF_RESET.
989      ENDIF.
990    *-----------------------------------------------------------------*
991      PERFORM CHECK_RFC_AMOUNT  .
992    *&-->  Check Amount
993      IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CREA' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'CHNG' OR
994         PREV_OKCODE = 'DISP' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'UNDEL' .
995      ENDIF.
996    *&--<
997    ENDMODULE.                 " check_docno  INPUT

1001   *      Exit command of screen 135
1002   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1003   MODULE EXIT_COMMAND_135 INPUT.
1004     OK_CODE =  SY-UCOMM.
1005     CASE OK_CODE .
1006       WHEN 'BACK' OR 'EXIT' OR 'CANCEL' .
1007         REFRESH CONTROL 'TC_135' FROM SCREEN '0135'.
1008         PERFORM RELEASE_LOCK.
1009         PERFORM CLEAR_SCREEN_0105.
1010         CLEAR OK_CODE .
1011         CLEAR PREV_OKCODE .

1013     ENDCASE.

1015   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1016   *&      Module  user_command_135  INPUT
1017   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1018   *      User command of screen - 135
1019   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1020   MODULE USER_COMMAND_135 INPUT.
1021     DATA: L_NO(2).
1022
1023     CLEAR: G_FIRET, G_MMRET.
1024
1025     PERFORM MODIFY_TABLE_EMDDTL .
1026     CLEAR G_STATUS.
1027     CASE OK_CODE.
1028   *&-----> Header Detail
1029       WHEN 'HEAD' .
1030         PERFORM DISP_HEAD USING WA_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1031                                 WA_EMDHDR-CRON
1032                                 WA_EMDHDR-TRANS.
1033         PREV_OKCODE = OK_CODE .
1034         CLEAR OK_CODE.
1035   *&-----> Send to Bank for Confirmation
1036       WHEN 'BCONF'.
1037         PERFORM CHECK_ITEM_SELECT  .
1038         IF G_ANS = '0'.
1039           PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING    TEXT-024 TEXT-025.
1040         ENDIF.
1041         IF G_ANS = 'J'.
1042           PERFORM UPDATE_FOR_BANKCONF .
1043           PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC_MAIN.
1044           MESSAGE S435(ZMM).
1045           PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_135.                           "-004
1046           LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100'.                             "-004
1047         ENDIF.
1048         PREV_OKCODE = OK_CODE .
1049         CLEAR OK_CODE.
1050   *&-----> Reset Document
1051       WHEN 'RESET'.
1052         PREV_OKCODE = OK_CODE .
1053         CLEAR OK_CODE.
1054         PERFORM CHECK_SEL_RESET_ITEM .
1055         IF G_ANS = '0' AND G_ERROR = 0.
1056           PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING    TEXT-026 TEXT-027.
1057           IF G_ANS = 'J'.
1058             PERFORM RESET_BANK_CONF_DOC USING G_IDOCNO G_ITEMNO
1059             G_RESET_STATUS  .
1060             PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC_MAIN.
1061             PERFORM STATUS_MESSAGE USING G_RESET_STATUS.
1062   *          perform release_lock.  -004
1063   *          perform clear_global_135. -004
1064   *          leave to screen '0100'.  -004
1065           ENDIF.
1066         ENDIF.
1067   *&------> Post Confirmation
1068       WHEN 'PCONF'.
1069         PERFORM CHECK_SEL_ITEM .
1070         IF G_ANS = '0' AND G_ERROR = 0.
1071           PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING    TEXT-028 TEXT-029.
1072           IF G_ANS = 'J'.
1073   *-----------------------------------------------------------*
1074   * Popup for Bank Deny/Confirm                               *
1075             PERFORM POPUP_DENY_ACCEPT.
1076             IF G_ANS_AD = '1' OR G_ANS_AD = '2'.
1077               PERFORM UPDATE_POST_CONF USING G_ANS_AD.
1078               PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC_MAIN.
1079               MESSAGE S439(ZMM).
1080   *           perform release_lock.      -004
1081   *            perform clear_global_135. -004
1082   *            leave to screen '0100'.   -004
1083             ENDIF.
1084           ENDIF.
1085         ENDIF.
1086   *&-------> Send Document to FI
1087       WHEN 'SNDFI'.
1088         PERFORM CHECK_SEL_ITEM_SNDFI .
1089         IF G_ANS = '0' AND G_ERROR = 0.
1090           PERFORM POPUP_CONFIRM USING    TEXT-041 TEXT-042.
1091           IF G_ANS = 'J'.
1092             PERFORM UPDATE_STATUS_SUBFI.
1093             PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC_MAIN.
1094             MESSAGE S449(ZMM).
1095   *          perform release_lock.
1096   *          perform clear_global_135. -004
1097   *          leave to screen '0100'.   -004
1098           ENDIF.
1099         ENDIF.
1100   *&-----> Document Amend
1101       WHEN 'AMEND'.
1102         IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO IS INITIAL.
1103           CALL SCREEN '0145'.
1104         ENDIF.
1105   *&-------> Item Details
1106       WHEN 'IDTL'.
1107         PERFORM GET_ITEM_DETAILS.
1108   *&------->  Invoke
1109       WHEN 'INVOK'.
1110   * Authorization Check  for INVOKING BG/ LC
1111         PERFORM  CHECK_AUTH .
1112         PERFORM CHECK_SEL_ITEM_INVOKE.
1113         IF G_ANS = '0' AND G_ERROR = 0.

1121           ENDIF.
1122         ENDIF.
1123   * Start of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009
1124         PERFORM  UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC_MAIN.
1125   * End of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009

1145           ENDIF.
1146         ENDIF.

1148           PERFORM RETRUN_DOCU.

1150   *        perform release_lock.     -004
1151   *        perform clear_global_135. -004

1286   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1287   *      User Command of screen 0145.
1288   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1289   MODULE USER_COMMAND_0145 INPUT.
1290     CASE OK_CODE .
1291       WHEN  'SAVE'.
1292         PERFORM SAVE_CHANGE_POPUP USING TEXT-006.
1293         IF G_SC_ANS = '1' .
1294   * Select record for the given document no. from DB table
1295           SELECT * INTO TABLE IST_DTL FROM ZMM_EMDDTL WHERE
1296                                      DOCNO = WA_EMDDTL-DOCNO  .
1297
1298           PERFORM MODIFY_DATA_S145  ON COMMIT  .
1299           IF G_SAVE_H = 0 AND G_SAVE_I = 0.
1300             COMMIT WORK.
1301             MESSAGE S455(ZMM).
1302             PERFORM CLEAR_GLOBAL_110.
1303             LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100' .
1304           ELSE.
1305             ROLLBACK WORK.
1306           ENDIF.
1307         ENDIF.
1308   *&-------> Item Details
1309       WHEN 'IDTL'.
1310         PERFORM GET_ITEM_DETAILS.
1311       WHEN 'HEAD' .
1312         PERFORM DISP_HEAD USING WA_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1313                                 WA_EMDHDR-CRON
1314                                 WA_EMDHDR-TRANS.
1315       WHEN  'ENMT' .
1316
1317         IF ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'TFS' OR
1318            ZMM_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'EMD'.
1319           SET PARAMETER ID 'ANF' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN.
1320           CALL TRANSACTION 'ME43' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
1321           CLEAR OK_CODE .
1322         ELSE.
1323
1324           CLEAR G_BSTYP .
1325           PERFORM GET_BSTYP.
1326           IF G_BSTYP = 'F'.
1327             SET PARAMETER ID 'BES' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
1328             CALL TRANSACTION 'ME23N' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
1329           ELSEIF G_BSTYP = 'K'.
1330             SET PARAMETER ID 'CTR' FIELD ZMM_EMDHDR-EBELN .
1331             CALL TRANSACTION 'ME33K' AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
1332           ENDIF.
1333           CLEAR OK_CODE .
1334         ENDIF.
1335       WHEN 'HSTRY' .
1336         LEAVE TO LIST-PROCESSING AND RETURN TO SCREEN 0145 .
1337         PERFORM DISP_CHG_HSTRY .
1338   * End of logic for changed document .
1339     ENDCASE.
1340   ENDMODULE.                 " user_command_0145  INPUT

1371   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1372   *&      Module  check_docno_s100  INPUT
1373   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1374   *      Check Document No entered in screen 100.
1375   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1376   MODULE CHECK_DOCNO_S100 INPUT.
1377     DATA L_MSG_TEXT(20).
1378     DATA L_INST_TYPE LIKE ZMM_EMDDTL-INST_TYPE .
1379     CLEAR WA_EMDHDR .
1380     IF   OK_CODE   = 'CHAN'.
1381       L_MSG_TEXT   = 'Change'.
1382     ELSEIF OK_CODE = 'DELE'.
1383       L_MSG_TEXT   = 'Delete'.
1384     ELSEIF OK_CODE = 'REST'.
1385       L_MSG_TEXT   = 'Reset'.
1386     ENDIF.
1387
1388     IF OK_CODE  = 'CHAN' OR
1389        OK_CODE  = 'DELE' OR
1390        OK_CODE  = 'BGLC' OR
1391        OK_CODE  = 'AMEND' .
1392       PERFORM CHECK_BGLC .
1393       IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO  IS INITIAL.
1394         SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
1395    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1396    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1397    ENDSELECT.
1398         IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
1399   *-----------------------------------------------------------------*
1400   *  For BG/LC header level Doc. complete validation is not applicable.
1401           SELECT INST_TYPE FROM ZMM_EMDDTL INTO L_INST_TYPE UP TO 1 ROWS
1402    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1403    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1404    ENDSELECT.
1405   *-----------------------------------------------------------------*
1406   *---> Check If the Document is Parked/Deleted.
1407           IF WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'D'.
1408             MESSAGE E425(ZMM).
1409           ELSEIF WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'U'.
1410             MESSAGE E470(ZMM) WITH   L_MSG_TEXT .
1411   *-- BG/LC doc. accepted by FI
1412           ELSEIF  WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'J' .
1413             IF ( L_INST_TYPE = 'BG' OR  L_INST_TYPE = 'LC' ) .
1414             ELSE.
1415               MESSAGE E499(ZMM) .
1416             ENDIF.
1417   *--- partially parked.
1418           ELSEIF   WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'K' .
1419             IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN' OR
1420                PREV_OKCODE = 'BGLC'.
1421             ELSE.
1422               MESSAGE E556(ZMM).
1423             ENDIF.
1424   *----- BG/LC Inprocess
1425           ELSEIF   WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'Q' .
1426             IF  ( L_INST_TYPE NE 'BG' OR  L_INST_TYPE NE 'LC' ) .
1427             ELSE.
1428               MESSAGE E557(ZMM).
1429             ENDIF.
1430   *+007 : Start
1431           ELSEIF   WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = '0' AND WA_EMDHDR-TRANS = 'SDT'.
1432             IF PREV_OKCODE = 'CHAN' OR PREV_OKCODE = 'DELE'.
1433             ELSE.
1434               IF  ( L_INST_TYPE EQ 'BG' OR  L_INST_TYPE EQ 'LC' ) .
1435                 MESSAGE E593(ZMM).
1436               ENDIF.
1437             ENDIF.
1438   *+007 : End
1439           ENDIF.
1440         ELSE.
1441           MESSAGE E411(ZMM).
1442         ENDIF.
1443   *------< End of check --------------------------------------*
1444       ENDIF.
1445     ENDIF.
1446     IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO  IS INITIAL AND NOT OK_CODE = 'BACK' .
1447       SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
1448    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1449    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1450    ENDSELECT.
1451       IF SY-SUBRC NE 0.                                       "+002
1452         MESSAGE E411(ZMM).
1453       ENDIF.                                                  "+002
1454     ENDIF.
1455     IF OK_CODE = 'REST'.
1456       IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO IS INITIAL.
1457         IF WA_EMDHDR-STATUS = 'N'.
1458           L_MSG_TEXT = TEXT-040.
1459           MESSAGE E472(ZMM).
1460         ELSEIF WA_EMDHDR-STATUS NE  'D'.
1461           MESSAGE E561(ZMM).
1462         ENDIF.
1463       ENDIF.
1464     ENDIF.
1465     IF NOT ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO IS INITIAL.
1466       IF PREV_OKCODE = 'AMEND'.
1467       ENDIF.
1468     ENDIF.
1469   ENDMODULE.                 " check_docno_s100  INPUT

1546   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1547   *&       UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC  INPUT
1548   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1549   *     Update Header Status based on BG/LC Doc. Actions.
1550   *   Eg. If all documents ar accepted by FI the Update Header status with
1551   *   Complete.
1552   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1553   MODULE UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC INPUT.
1554     DATA: WA_DTL_T001 TYPE ZMM_EMD_STATUS.
1555     DATA: L_STAT,
1556           L_FOUND.
1557     LOOP AT IST_EMDDTL INTO WA_DTL_T001.
1558       IF SY-TABIX = 1.
1559         L_STAT    =  WA_DTL_T001-STATUS.
1560       ENDIF.
1561       IF L_STAT   NE  WA_DTL_T001-STATUS.
1562         L_FOUND   =  'X'.
1563         EXIT.
1564       ELSE.
1565         L_FOUND   =  '0'.
1566       ENDIF.
1567     ENDLOOP.
1568     IF L_FOUND = '0' AND NOT L_STAT IS INITIAL.
1569       IF L_STAT ='N'.
1570         PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC USING G_IDOCNO 'N'.
1571       ELSEIF L_STAT = 'P'.
1572         PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC USING G_IDOCNO 'J'.
1573       ENDIF.
1574     ELSEIF L_FOUND = 'X'.
1575       PERFORM UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC USING G_IDOCNO 'Q'.
1576     ENDIF.
1577   ENDMODULE.                 " UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS_BGLC  INPUT
1578   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

358        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
359        MOVE 'RESET'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
360        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
361        MOVE 'SAVE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
362        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab     .
363        MOVE 'HEAD'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
364        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
365        MOVE 'DOCU'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
366        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
367        MOVE 'IDTL'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
368        APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
369      ENDIF.
370      IF prev_okcode = 'DISP' OR prev_okcode = 'DELE'.
371        MOVE 'SAVE'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
372        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
373        MOVE  'RESET'  TO  wa_tab-fcode .
374        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
375      ENDIF.
376      IF prev_okcode = 'REST'.
377        REFRESH ist_tab .
378        MOVE 'SAVE'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
379        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
380        MOVE 'DELT'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
381        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
382      ENDIF.
383
384      IF prev_okcode = 'CREA' OR prev_okcode = 'ECREA' OR       "+002
385         prev_okcode = 'CHAN' OR
386        prev_okcode = 'DISP'.
387        MOVE  'RESET'  TO  wa_tab-fcode .
388        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
389        MOVE 'DELT'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
390        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
391      ENDIF.
392      IF prev_okcode = 'DELE' .
393        MOVE 'RESET'   TO  wa_tab-fcode .
394        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
395      ENDIF.
396      IF prev_okcode = 'DELE' AND ok_code = 'DELT'.
397        MOVE 'DELE'    TO  wa_tab-fcode  .
398        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
399      ENDIF.
400      IF prev_okcode = 'CHAN'.
401        IF g_reset  = 0.
402          MOVE 'RESET'   TO  wa_tab-fcode .
403          APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
404          MOVE 'DELT'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
405          APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
406        ENDIF.
407      ENDIF.
408      IF  zmm_emdhdr-ebeln IS INITIAL.
409    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
410    *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
411         MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
412    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
413
414        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
415        MOVE 'ME43'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
416        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
417      ELSE.
418      ENDIF.
419      IF prev_okcode = 'REST'.
420        MOVE 'SAVE'   TO  wa_tab-fcode .
421        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
422        MOVE 'DELT'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
423        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab      .
424      ENDIF.
425
426      IF ( zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS' OR  zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD' ) AND NOT
427    zmm_emdhdr-ebeln IS INITIAL .
428    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
429    *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
430         MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
431    "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
432        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab    .
433      ELSEIF (  zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT' )
434      AND NOT zmm_emdhdr-ebeln IS INITIAL.
435        MOVE 'ME43'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
436        APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
437      ENDIF.
438    ENDFORM.                    " MODIFY_ISTTAB

655          MOVE 'SRM' TO wa_emdhdr-trtyp.

667        ENDIF.

670          SORT ist_del_emddtl BY item_no .
671        ENDIF.
672        REFRESH: ist_del_emddtl .
673        IF NOT ist_emddtl01 IS INITIAL.
674          MODIFY zmm_emddtl FROM TABLE ist_emddtl01.
675          IF sy-subrc = 0.
676            g_save_i = 0.
677          ELSE.
678            g_save_i = 1.
679          ENDIF.
680        ELSE.
681          g_save_i = 1.
682        ENDIF.
683      ENDIF.

687    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
688    *&      Form  insert_itemdtl
689    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
690    *    Insert Item Data into Internal Table ist_emddtl01
691    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
692    *  -->  p1        text
693    *  <--  p2        text
694    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
695    FORM insert_itemdtl.
696      REFRESH ist_emddtl01.
697      CLEAR  wa_emddtl01  .
698      CLEAR  g_amount .
699      DATA : l_bstyp .
700      "commented by lipsy on 09.08.2012 for addition of etender
701                                                                "RD1K981279
702    *  IF prev_okcode =  'CREA' OR  prev_okcode = 'CHAN'.
703                                                                "RD1K981279
704      "end of comment by lipsy on 09.08.2012 for addition of etender
705      ""added by lipsy on 09.08.2012 for addition of etender
706                                                                "RD1K981279
707      IF prev_okcode =  'CREA' OR  prev_okcode = 'CHAN' OR prev_okcode =  'ECREA'.
708                                                                "RD1K981279
709        "end of addition  by lipsy on 09.08.2012 for addition of etender
710        IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS' OR zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD' .
711          MOVE  'A' TO  wa_emdhdr-bstyp  .
712        ELSEIF  zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT' .
713          SELECT SINGLE bstyp FROM ekko INTO l_bstyp
714           WHERE ebeln = zmm_emdhdr-ebeln.
715          MOVE l_bstyp  TO wa_emdhdr-bstyp .
716        ENDIF.
717      ENDIF.
718      IF prev_okcode =  'CREA' .
719        MOVE 'N'  TO  wa_emdhdr-status .
720      ENDIF.
721      LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl .
722        g_amount  = g_amount + wa_emddtl-amount    .
723        MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emddtl TO    wa_emddtl01           .
724        IF wa_emddtl-status IS INITIAL.
725          MOVE 'N'                  TO    wa_emddtl01-status    .
726        ELSE.
727          MOVE wa_emddtl-status     TO   wa_emddtl01-status  .
728        ENDIF.
729        MOVE g_docno                 TO    wa_emddtl01-docno     .
730        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-trans        TO    wa_emddtl01-trans     .
731        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-ebeln        TO    wa_emddtl01-ebeln     .
732        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-vendorno     TO    wa_emddtl01-vendno    .
733        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-tenderno     TO    wa_emddtl01-tendno    .
734        MOVE wa_emdhdr-bstyp         TO    wa_emddtl01-bstyp      .
735        MOVE space                   TO    wa_emddtl01-fi_parkno  .
736        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-currency     TO    wa_emddtl01-currency   .
737        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-ebidno       TO    wa_emddtl01-ebidno . "+002
738    * Begin of <RD1K973923> on 20102010
739        MOVE zmm_emdhdr-co_code      TO    wa_emddtl01-co_code .
740    * End of <RD1K973923>
741        wa_emddtl01-crby = sy-uname .
742        wa_emddtl01-cron = sy-datum .
743    *-------------------------------------------------------------*
744    ** SRM Changes +001
745        IF prev_okcode = 'ECREA' OR g_ttype = 'SRM'.
746          MOVE 'SRM' TO wa_emddtl01-trtyp.
747        ELSEIF prev_okcode = 'CREA'.
748          MOVE 'R3' TO wa_emddtl01-trtyp.
749        ENDIF.
750    *-------------------------------------------------------------*
751        IF prev_okcode = 'CREA' OR prev_okcode = 'ECREA'.
752          MOVE g_docno               TO    wa_emddtl01-docno   .
753        ELSEIF prev_okcode = 'CHAN'.
754          MOVE g_idocno              TO    wa_emddtl01-docno  .
755        ENDIF.
756        IF prev_okcode = 'CHAN'.
757          wa_emddtl01-chby = sy-uname.
758          wa_emddtl01-chon = sy-datum.
759          wa_emddtl01-docno = g_idocno.
760        ENDIF.
761        APPEND  wa_emddtl01 TO ist_emddtl01 .
762      ENDLOOP.
763    ENDFORM.                    " insert_itemdtl
764    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
765    *&      Form  calc_total_amount
766    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
767    *      Claculate  Total Amount at Header Level
768
769    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
770    *  -->  p1        text
771    *  <--  p2        text
772    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
773    FORM calc_total_amount.
774      CLEAR wa_emddtl.
775      CLEAR g_amount .
776      LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl.
777        g_amount =  g_amount +  wa_emddtl-amount .
778      ENDLOOP.

780      MOVE g_amount TO wa_emdhdr-amount   .
781      MOVE zmm_emdhdr-place TO wa_emdhdr-place .
782    ENDFORM.                    " calc_total_amount
783    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
784    *&      Form  GET_DONONO
785    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
786    *      Get Document no using number range Obj. ZMM_EMDDOC.
787    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
788    *  -->  p1        text
789    *  <--  p2        text
790    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
791    FORM get_docno.
792      CLEAR g_docno .
793
794      IF g_okhdr IS INITIAL.
795        CALL FUNCTION 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
796          EXPORTING
797            nr_range_nr             = '01'
798            object                  = 'ZMM_EMDDOC'
799            quantity                = '1'
800            toyear                  = sy-datum+0(4)
801          IMPORTING
802            number                  = g_docno
803          EXCEPTIONS
804            interval_not_found      = 1
805            number_range_not_intern = 2
806            object_not_found        = 3
807            quantity_is_0           = 4
808            quantity_is_not_1       = 5
809            interval_overflow       = 6
810            buffer_overflow         = 7
811            OTHERS                  = 8.
812        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
813          MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
814                  WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
815        ENDIF.
816      ENDIF.
817    ENDFORM.                    " GET_DOCNO
818    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
819    *&      Form  POPUP_CONFIRM

821    *    Popup Message When 'SAVE' or 'CHANGE'.

826    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
827    FORM popup_confirm USING    p_text_01
828                                p_text_02 .
829
830      CLEAR g_ans.
831      " Begin of <RD1K960036>.
832    *  call function 'POPUP_TO_CONFIRM_STEP'
833    *       exporting
834    *            defaultoption = 'Y'
835    *            textline1     = p_text_02
836    *            titel         = p_text_01
837    *            start_column  = 20
838    *            start_row     = 6

859            MOVE 'N' TO g_ans.
860        ENDCASE.
861      ENDIF.
862      " End of <RD1K960036>.

864    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
865    *&      Form  get_data
866    *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
867    *       Get Data for Display,Change & Delete
868    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
869    *  -->  p1        text
870    *  <--  p2        text
871    *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
872    FORM get_data.
873      REFRESH  ist_emddtl    .
874      CLEAR :  ist_emddtl    ,
875               wa_emdhdr     ,
876               wa_emddtl01   ,
877               g_status      ,
878               g_reset       ,
879               g_dansw       ,
880               g_reset       .
881      IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-docno IS INITIAL.
882        SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
883     WHERE DOCNO = G_IDOCNO
884     ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
885     ENDSELECT.
886        IF sy-subrc = 0.
887    *&--> Display Status using variable g_status.
888          IF wa_emdhdr-status = 'N'.
889            g_status  = 'Created'.
890          ELSEIF wa_emdhdr-status = 'D'.
891            IF prev_okcode = 'DELE'.
892              MESSAGE e425(zmm).
893            ELSE.
894              g_status  = 'Deleted'.
895            ENDIF.
896          ELSE.
897            g_reset   = 0.
898          ENDIF.
899    *&--<
900          IF wa_emdhdr-status = 'D'.
901            MESSAGE w416(zmm) WITH  wa_emdhdr-docno.
902            g_reset = 1.
903          ENDIF.
904    *&-----> Get Place NAme from table zmm_location
905          SELECT SINGLE locds FROM zmm_location INTO g_locname
906                 WHERE loccd  = wa_emdhdr-place .
907    *&-------> Get Item Details  based on Docno and transaction.
908          MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emdhdr TO zmm_emdhdr .
909          SELECT * FROM zmm_emddtl INTO TABLE ist_emddtl01
910                   WHERE docno = wa_emdhdr-docno  AND
911                         trans = wa_emdhdr-trans  ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
912          IF sy-subrc = 0.

1162   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1163   *  -->  p1        text
1164   *  <--  p2        text
1165   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1166   FORM check_inst_type USING p_inst_type.
1167   * +004 Payment Gateway SRM Changes.
1168     IF prev_okcode NE 'ECREA' AND  p_inst_type = 'OP' .
1169   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1170       CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1171   *End of <RD1K963111>
1172       MESSAGE e589(zmm).
1173     ENDIF.
1174
1175     IF prev_okcode = 'ECREA' AND p_inst_type = 'OP'.
1176       IF zmm_emdhdr-trans NE 'TFS'.
1177   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1178         CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1179   *End of <RD1K963111>
1180         MESSAGE e590(zmm).
1181       ENDIF.
1182     ENDIF.
1183   *--+004
1184   *&--> Instrument IP is not allowed for EMD AND SD
1185
1186     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD' OR zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
1187       IF p_inst_type = 'IP'.
1188   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1189         CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1190   *End of <RD1K963111>
1191         MESSAGE e423(zmm) WITH zmm_emdhdr-trans .
1192       ENDIF.
1193     ENDIF.
1194   *---- Instrument type BG/LC is not allowed in case of Tender Fee
1195     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
1196       IF p_inst_type = 'BG' OR
1197          p_inst_type = 'LC'.
1198   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1199         CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1200   *End of <RD1K963111>
1201         MESSAGE e550(zmm).
1202       ENDIF.
1203     ENDIF.
1204
1205     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans =  'EMD' OR
1206        zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
1207       IF p_inst_type = 'IV'.
1208   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1209         CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1210   *End of <RD1K963111>
1211         MESSAGE e481(zmm).
1212       ENDIF.
1213     ENDIF.
1214   *Begin of <RD1K963111>
1215     IF prev_okcode = 'ECREA' AND wa_tc105-inst_type = 'IP'.
1216       CLEAR wa_tc105-inst_type.
1217       MESSAGE e865(zmm).
1218     ENDIF.
1219   *&--<
1220     DATA lv_index TYPE i.
1221     DATA lv_ins_type TYPE zmm_emddtl-inst_type.
1222     DATA lv_mesg TYPE string.
1223     DESCRIBE TABLE ist_emddtl LINES lv_index.
1224     IF lv_index > 1.
1225       READ TABLE ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl INDEX 1.
1226       lv_ins_type = wa_emddtl-inst_type.
1227       IF lv_ins_type NE wa_tc105-inst_type.
1228         IF lv_ins_type = 'CC'.
1229           IF wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'CC' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'BC' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'DD'.
1230           ELSE.
1231             MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1232           ENDIF.
1233         ELSEIF lv_ins_type = 'BC'.
1234           IF wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'BC' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'CC' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'DD'.
1235           ELSE.
1236             MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1237           ENDIF.
1238
1239         ELSEIF lv_ins_type = 'DD'.
1240           IF wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'DD' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'BC' OR wa_tc105-inst_type EQ 'CC'.
1241           ELSE.
1242             MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1243           ENDIF.
1244
1245         ELSEIF lv_ins_type = 'IP' AND lv_ins_type NE wa_tc105-inst_type.
1246           MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1247
1248         ELSEIF lv_ins_type = 'BG' AND lv_ins_type NE wa_tc105-inst_type.
1249           MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1250
1251         ELSEIF lv_ins_type = 'LC' AND lv_ins_type NE wa_tc105-inst_type.
1252           MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1253
1254         ENDIF.
1255       ENDIF.
1256     ENDIF.
1257   *End of <RD1K963111>
1258   * End of modification by SAB_SARVANAN on 09/04/2009 - RD1K963111
1259   ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_INST_TYPE

1323   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1324   *&      Form  copy_line
1325   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1326   *     Copy Line Item Data
1327   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1328   *  -->  p1        text
1329   *  <--  p2        text
1330   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1331   FORM copy_line.
1332     REFRESH : ist_emddtl_t01 .
1333     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X'.
1334       MOVE space  TO wa_emddtl-sel .
1335       APPEND  wa_emddtl  TO ist_emddtl_t01 .
1336       wa_emddtl-sel = space.
1337       MODIFY ist_emddtl FROM wa_emddtl.
1338     ENDLOOP.
1339     CLEAR ok_code.
1340   ENDFORM.                    " copy_line
1341   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1342   *&      Form  PASTE_LINE
1343   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1344   *       Paste Selected Line Item.
1345   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1346   *  -->  p1        text
1347   *  <--  p2        text
1348   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1349   FORM paste_line.
1350
1351     DATA: l_line LIKE zmm_emddtl-item_no.
1352     DESCRIBE TABLE ist_emddtl LINES l_line .
1353     LOOP AT ist_emddtl_t01 INTO wa_emddtl  .
1354       l_line = l_line + 1.
1355       MOVE l_line  TO  wa_emddtl-item_no.
1356       MOVE 'N' TO wa_emddtl-status .
1357       MOVE space TO wa_emddtl-sel   .
1358       APPEND  wa_emddtl  TO ist_emddtl  .
1359     ENDLOOP.
1360
1361     DESCRIBE TABLE ist_emddtl LINES l_line .
1362     tc_105-lines = l_line .
1363     REFRESH ist_emddtl_t01.
1364     CLEAR ok_code.
1365   ENDFORM.                    " PASTE_LINE

1391   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1392   *&      Form  check_inst_group
1393   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1394   *    Check Instrurment Group
1395   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1396   *  -->  p1        text
1397   *  <--  p2        text
1398   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1399   FORM check_inst_group.
1400     DATA: ist_emddtl_temp LIKE TABLE OF wa_emddtl.
1401   *&--> Check Whether Instrument Type entered is from same Group or not
1402   *&--> G1- DD,BC,CC G2-LC , G3-BG , G4-IP
1403
1404   **** check for Instrument type. This logic is laid for the reason
1405   ***** that no
1406   ***** (1) If Instrument type is DD then only BC and CC is permitted
1407   ***** (2) If Instrument is BG then only BG is permitted.
1408   ****  (3) IF Instrument is IV then only IV is permitted.
1409
1410   ** New Logic for Instrument Group added on 09.03.05 *********
1411
1412
1413   ** End of Addition ******************************************
1414
1415     DATA: ist_instgrp TYPE TABLE OF zmm_emddtl-inst_type.
1416     DATA:  wa_instgrp TYPE zmm_emddtl-inst_type.
1417     DATA: l_inst(20).
1418     DATA: l_fd  .
1419     REFRESH ist_instgrp.
1420     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl.
1421       wa_instgrp  = wa_emddtl-inst_type.
1422       APPEND wa_instgrp  TO ist_instgrp.
1423     ENDLOOP.
1424     SORT ist_instgrp .
1425     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ist_instgrp .
1426     LOOP AT ist_instgrp INTO wa_instgrp .
1427       CONCATENATE  l_inst
1428      wa_instgrp  INTO l_inst .
1429     ENDLOOP.
1430
1431     IF NOT l_inst IS INITIAL.
1432
1433       IF l_inst  = 'BCCCDD'  OR
1434          l_inst  = 'BCCC'    OR
1435          l_inst  = 'CCDD'    OR
1436          l_inst  = 'CCBC'    OR
1437          l_inst  = 'BCDD'    OR
1438          l_inst  = 'DD'      OR
1439          l_inst  = 'CC'      OR
1440          l_inst  = 'BC'      OR
1441          l_inst  = 'BG'      OR
1442          l_inst  = 'IP'      OR
1443          l_inst  = 'IV'      OR
1444          l_inst   = 'LC' or
1445          l_inst  = 'BT'.
1446         l_fd = 'X'.
1447         g_error = 1 .
1448       ELSE.
1449         g_error = 0.
1450         MESSAGE i426(zmm).
1451       ENDIF.
1452     ENDIF.
1453   ENDFORM.                    " check_inst_group
1454   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1455   *&      Form  SAVE_DATA_110
1456   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1457   *       Save Data in screen 110. REFUND/FORFEIT/EMD-SD CONVERSION
1458   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1459   *  -->  p1        text
1460   *  <--  p2        text
1461   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1462   FORM save_data_110.
1463     g_save_h = 1.
1464     g_save_i = 1.
1465
1466   *  data l_status .
1467     DATA: l_tot_item LIKE sy-tabix.
1468     DATA: ist_emdref_tmp TYPE TABLE OF  zmm_emdref.
1469   * Begin of <RD1K963111>
1470     DATA: its_emddtl   TYPE TABLE OF zmm_emddtl,
1471           wa_emddtl_01 TYPE zmm_emddtl.
1472   * End of <RD1K963111>
1473     IF sy-subrc = 0.
1474       g_save_h = 0.
1475     ELSE.
1476       g_save_h = 1.
1477     ENDIF.
1478   *&--<
1479     l_tot_item  = 1.
1480     MOVE-CORRESPONDING zmm_emdref TO wa_emdref   .
1481     IF g_docstat = 'C'.
1482       zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
1483     ENDIF.
1484     MOVE zmm_emdhdr-docno  TO wa_emdref-refdoc   .
1485     MOVE zmm_emdhdr-trans  TO wa_emdref-trans    .
1486     IF  g_docstat =  'C'.
1487       MOVE zmm_emdref-ebeln  TO wa_emdref-ebeln    .
1488     ELSE.
1489       MOVE zmm_emdhdr-ebeln  TO wa_emdref-ebeln    .
1490     ENDIF.
1491     IF NOT  g_ebeln IS INITIAL .
1492       MOVE g_ebeln  TO wa_emdref-ebeln    .
1493     ENDIF.
1494   * Begin of <RD1K963111>
1495     SELECT * FROM zmm_emddtl INTO TABLE its_emddtl WHERE docno = zmm_emdhdr-docno AND ebeln = wa_emdref-ebeln.
1496     LOOP AT its_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl_01.
1497       IF g_docstat = 'R'.
1498         wa_emddtl_01-refdt = sy-datum.
1499       ELSEIF g_docstat = 'C'.
1500         wa_emddtl_01-candt = sy-datum.
1501       ELSEIF g_docstat = 'F'.
1502         wa_emddtl_01-fofdt = sy-datum.
1503       ENDIF.
1504       MODIFY zmm_emddtl FROM wa_emddtl_01.
1505     ENDLOOP.
1506   * End of <RD1K963111>
1507     MOVE zmm_emdhdr-docno  TO wa_emdref-refdoc   .
1508     MOVE g_docstat         TO wa_emdref-status   .
1509     MOVE sy-uname          TO wa_emdref-rfccrby  .
1510     MOVE sy-datum          TO wa_emdref-rfccron  .
1511     MOVE g_docno           TO wa_emdref-docno    .
1512     MOVE l_tot_item        TO wa_emdref-itemno   .
1513     MOVE space             TO wa_emdref-fi_parkno .
1514     MOVE zmm_emdhdr-currency TO wa_emdref-currency.
1515     MODIFY zmm_emdref FROM wa_emdref.
1516   * Begin of <RD1K963111>
1517     zmm_emdhdr-ref_doc = g_docno.
1518     MODIFY zmm_emdhdr.
1519   * End of <RD1K963111>
1520     IF sy-subrc NE 0.
1521       g_save_h = 1.
1522       MESSAGE e427(zmm).
1523     ELSE.
1524       g_save_h = 0.
1525     ENDIF.
1526   ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_DATA_110

1561   *&      Form  CHECK_DOCNO
1562   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1563   *       Validate input Docno
1564   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1565   *  -->  p1        text
1566   *  <--  p2        text
1567   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1568   FORM check_docno.
1569     DATA :  wa_emdhdr_tmp LIKE zmm_emdhdr,
1570             wa_emdref_t01 LIKE zmm_emdref.
1571     CLEAR g_ref.
1572     CLEAR wa_emdhdr .
1573
1574     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR_T02 UP TO 1 ROWS
1575    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1576    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1577    ENDSELECT.
1578     IF sy-subrc NE 0.
1579       SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF
1580    INTO WA_EMDREF_T01 UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
1581    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1582    ENDSELECT.
1583       IF sy-subrc NE 0.
1584         g_ref =  1.
1585         MESSAGE e411(zmm).
1586       ELSE.
1587
1588         SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR_TMP UP TO 1 ROWS
1589    WHERE DOCNO = WA_EMDREF_T01-REFDOC
1590    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1591    ENDSELECT.
1592
1593         MOVE wa_emdref_t01-amount TO wa_emdhdr_tmp-amount.
1594         MOVE  wa_emdref_t01-trans TO wa_emdhdr_tmp-trans .
1595         MOVE  zmm_emdhdr-docno           TO wa_emdhdr_tmp-docno.
1596         MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emdhdr_tmp TO zmm_emdhdr.
1597         g_ref = 0.
1598       ENDIF.
1599     ELSE.
1600       g_ref = 0.
1601       IF wa_emdhdr_t02-status = 'O'.
1602         MESSAGE e230(zmm).
1603       ENDIF.
1604       MOVE wa_emdhdr_t02-currency       TO zmm_emdref-currency .
1605     ENDIF.
1606
1607     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS' AND zmm_emdref-rscode = '120' AND sy-dynnr = '0110'.
1608       zmm_emdref-amount = zmm_emdhdr-amount.
1609     ENDIF.
1610
1611
1612   ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_DOCNO

1785   FORM append_isttab_115.
1786
1787     REFRESH  ist_tab   .
1788     IF prev_okcode = 'CRET' OR
1789        prev_okcode = 'CHNG' OR
1790        prev_okcode = 'DISP' OR

1799       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1800       MOVE 'DELE'   TO wa_tab-fcode.
1801       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1802       MOVE 'HEAD'   TO wa_tab-fcode.
1803       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1804       MOVE 'RESET'  TO wa_tab-fcode.
1805       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1806   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1807   *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1808        MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1809   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1810       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab    .
1811       MOVE 'ME43'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1812       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab    .
1813       MOVE 'ENMT'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1814       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
1815     ENDIF.
1816     IF prev_okcode = 'DISP' OR prev_okcode = 'DELE' .
1817       REFRESH  ist_tab .
1818       MOVE 'SAVE' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1819       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1820       MOVE 'HEAD' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1821       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1822       MOVE 'RFCD' TO wa_tab-fcode.
1823       APPEND wa_tab TO ist_tab  .
1824       MOVE 'ME43'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1825       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
1826   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1827   *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1828        MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1829   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1830       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
1831       MOVE 'ENMT'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1832       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
1833       MOVE 'RESET'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1834       APPEND wa_tab  TO  ist_tab.
1835     ENDIF.
1836     IF prev_okcode = 'UNDEL'.
1837       DELETE  ist_tab WHERE fcode = 'RESET'.
1838     ENDIF.

1846       APPEND wa_tab  TO   ist_tab  .

1848       APPEND wa_tab  TO   ist_tab  .

1850       APPEND wa_tab  TO   ist_tab  .
1851       MOVE 'ME43'    TO   wa_tab-fcode .
1852       APPEND wa_tab  TO   ist_tab.
1853   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1854   *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1855        MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1856   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC

1877   zmm_emdhdr-ebeln IS INITIAL .
1878   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
1879   *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1880        MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
1881   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC

1903   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1904   *&      Form  save_chg_docu
1905   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1906   *       Subroutine being used for saving change document iinformation
1907   *       in CDPOS and CDHDR
1908   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1909   *  -->  p1        text
1910   *  <--  p2        text
1911   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1912   FORM save_chg_docu.
1913
1914     DATA  : l_objectid LIKE cdhdr-objectid .
1915     DATA  : old_emdhdr LIKE zmm_emdhdr .
1916     DATA  : BEGIN OF l_place OCCURS 0 .
1917             INCLUDE STRUCTURE cdtxt.
1918     DATA  : END OF l_place .
1919
1920     old_emdhdr = wa_emdhdr .
1921     wa_emdhdr-place = zmm_emdhdr-place .
1922
1923     l_objectid = wa_emdhdr-docno .
1924
1925     CALL FUNCTION 'ZPLACE_WRITE_DOCUMENT'
1926       EXPORTING
1927         objectid       = l_objectid
1928         tcode          = sy-tcode
1929         utime          = sy-uzeit
1930         udate          = sy-datum
1931         username       = sy-uname
1932         n_zmm_emdhdr   = wa_emdhdr
1933         o_zmm_emdhdr   = old_emdhdr
1934         upd_zmm_emdhdr = 'U'
1935       TABLES
1936         icdtxt_zplace  = l_place.
1937   ENDFORM.                    " save_chg_docu
1938   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1939   *&      Form  check_docno_rfc
1940   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
1941   *      Get Document
1942   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1943   *  -->  p1        text
1944   *  <--  p2        text
1945   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
1946   FORM check_docno_rfc.
1947
1948     CLEAR g_rfc_chk.
1949     CLEAR wa_emdhdr .
1950     CLEAR g_docno .
1951     DATA: l_tendno LIKE zmm_emddtl-tendno,
1952           l_vendno LIKE zmm_emddtl-vendno,
1953           l_ekgrp  LIKE zmm_emdhdr-ekgrp.
1954
1955     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF UP TO 1 ROWS
1956
1957    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO
1958    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
1959    ENDSELECT.
1960
1961     IF sy-subrc = 0.
1962   *&----->  Raise Error Message if Document is deleted.
1963
1964       IF  ( zmm_emdref-status = 'D' ) AND ( prev_okcode = 'CHNG' OR
1965           prev_okcode = 'DELE' ).
1966         g_rfc_chk = '1'.
1967
1968         MESSAGE e425(zmm).
1969       ELSE.
1970         g_rfc_chk = 0.
1971       ENDIF.
1972   *-----------------------------------------------------------*
1973   * Check whether Document Submitted to FI . If the raise error.
1974   *-----------------------------------------------------------*
1975       IF prev_okcode  NE 'DISP'.
1976         IF zmm_emdref-status = 'S' .
1977           g_rfc_chk = '1'.
1978           MESSAGE e488(zmm).
1979         ENDIF.
1980       ENDIF.
1981   *----- If Document status is 'C' Don;t allow to Reset.*------
1982       IF ok_code = 'UNDEL'.
1983         IF zmm_emdref-status = 'R' OR
1984            zmm_emdref-status = 'F' OR
1985            zmm_emdref-status = 'C'.
1986           g_rfc_chk = '1'.
1987           MESSAGE e483(zmm).
1988         ENDIF.
1989       ENDIF.
1990   *-----------------------------------------------------------*
1991       IF ok_code = 'UNDEL'.
1992         PERFORM check_doc_status  USING zmm_emdref-docno.
1993       ENDIF.
1994   *&------<
1995       g_amt = zmm_emdref-amount .
1996       g_docno = zmm_emdref-docno.
1997
1998       g_rfc_chk = 0.
1999       SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR_T02 UP TO 1 ROWS
2000    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-REFDOC
2001    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
2002    ENDSELECT.
2003       IF sy-subrc = 0.
2004         MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emdhdr_t02 TO zmm_emdhdr .
2005   *      MOVE zmm_emdhdr-balamt TO g_balamt .
2006   *----------------------------------------------------------------*
2007   * if Ref. document is not in header then go for ZMM_EMDREF TABLE
2008   *  COMPARE docno with refdoc no in same table and status = 'C'.
2009   *  This is for getting Receipt total amount of Converted Document.
2010   *----------------------------------------------------------------*
2011       ELSE.
2012
2013         SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF INTO CORRESPONDING
2014    FIELDS OF WA_EMDHDR_T02 UP TO 1 ROWS WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-REFDOC AND RFCSTAT = 'C'
2015    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
2016    ENDSELECT.
2017         MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emdhdr_t02 TO zmm_emdhdr .
2018         PERFORM get_tendno_vendno USING wa_emdhdr_t02-ebeln
2019       CHANGING l_tendno l_vendno l_ekgrp  .
2020
2021         MOVE l_tendno TO zmm_emdhdr-tenderno.
2022         MOVE l_vendno TO zmm_emdhdr-vendorno.
2023         MOVE l_ekgrp    TO zmm_emdhdr-ekgrp .
2024       ENDIF.
2025     ELSE.
2026       g_rfc_chk = 1.
2027       MESSAGE e411(zmm).
2028     ENDIF.
2029     MOVE zmm_emdhdr-currency   TO zmm_emdref-currency .
2030   ENDFORM.                    " check_docno_rfc

2054   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2055   *&      Form  check_rfc_amount
2056   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2057   *       Check Amount Entered for Refund/Forfeit/EMD-SD Conv.
2058   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2059   *  -->  p1        text
2060   *  <--  p2        text
2061   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2062   FORM check_rfc_amount.
2063
2064     DATA: l_tamt LIKE zmm_emdhdr-amount .
2065     DATA: l_act_bal LIKE zmm_emdhdr-amount .
2066   *&---> Check Refund Amount
2067     PERFORM get_total_ref  .
2068   *&---<
2069     CLEAR : g_balamt.
2070     g_balamt = zmm_emdhdr-amount  -  g_tot_ref .
2071
2072     l_act_bal = zmm_emdhdr-amount -  g_tot_ref .
2073
2074     IF prev_okcode = 'CREA'.
2075       g_amt = 0.
2076     ENDIF.
2077   *----------------------------------------------*
2078     IF g_rfc  = 0  AND g_amt NE zmm_emdref-amount .
2079
2080   *&----> If Amount Entered is less than Doc. amount Add diff. amt to
2081   * Balance amount .
2082       IF zmm_emdref-amount < g_amt .
2083         g_balamt =  g_amt -  zmm_emdref-amount + g_balamt.
2084       ELSEIF  zmm_emdref-amount > g_amt.
2085         l_tamt = zmm_emdref-amount - g_amt .
2086         IF   l_tamt > l_act_bal.
2087           MESSAGE e429(zmm).
2088         ELSE.
2089           g_balamt = g_balamt - ( zmm_emdref-amount - g_amt ) .
2090         ENDIF.
2091       ELSE.
2092         g_balamt    =  g_balamt   - zmm_emdref-amount.
2093       ENDIF.
2094     ENDIF.                                                    "20.02.2004
2095   *&--<
2096   ENDFORM.                    " check_rfc_amount
2097   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

2099   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2100   *      Get Total Balance Amount from Refund Table ZMM_EMDREF
2101   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2102   *  -->  p1        text
2103   *  <--  p2        text
2104   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2105   FORM get_total_ref .
2106     g_tot_ref  =  0.
2107     CLEAR g_tot_ref .
2108     SELECT * FROM zmm_emdref INTO TABLE ist_emdref
2109                WHERE     refdoc  = zmm_emdhdr-docno AND
2110                         status IN ('R','F','C','I','S').
2111
2112     IF sy-subrc = 0.
2113       LOOP AT ist_emdref INTO wa_emdref.
2114         g_tot_ref = g_tot_ref + wa_emdref-amount.
2115       ENDLOOP.
2116     ENDIF.
2117   ENDFORM.                    " get_total_balamt
