2434   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2435   *&      Form  append_isttab_135
2436   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2437   *      Append F-code to ist_tab.
2438   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2439   *  -->  p1        text
2440   *  <--  p2        text
2441   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2442   FORM append_isttab_135.
2443     DATA: wa_dtl  LIKE wa_emddtl.
2444     REFRESH ist_tab .
2445     DATA: l_found LIKE sy-tabix.
2446     DATA l_lines LIKE sy-tabix .
2447     DATA : l_bstyp TYPE zmm_emdhdr-bstyp.
2448
2449     SELECT SINGLE  bstyp FROM ekko INTO l_bstyp
2450              WHERE  ebeln = zmm_emdhdr-ebeln .
2451
2452     IF l_bstyp = 'F' OR l_bstyp = 'K'.
2453       MOVE 'ME43'        TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2454       APPEND wa_tab      TO  ist_tab      .
2455     ELSEIF l_bstyp = 'A'.
2456   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **BEGIN OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
2457   *    MOVE 'ME23'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2458        MOVE 'ME23N'    TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2459   "Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **END OF CHANGE BY SAP_ABAP 11.06.2026 FOR ATC
2460       APPEND wa_tab      TO  ist_tab      .
2461     ENDIF.
2462
2463     MOVE 'SAVE'        TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2464     APPEND wa_tab      TO  ist_tab      .
2465     IF prev_okcode = 'RESET'  .
2466       MOVE 'BCONF'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2467       APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2468       MOVE 'SAVE'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2469       APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2470
2471     ELSEIF prev_okcode =   'BCONF'.
2472       MOVE 'RESET'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2473       APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2474       MOVE 'SAVE'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2475       APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2476     ENDIF.
2477
2478   *---- If instrument type is 'LC' Disable icon "SND TO BNK" & "POST CNF".
2479     IF set_okcode = 'BGLC'.
2480       IF NOT ist_emddtl IS INITIAL.
2481         READ TABLE ist_emddtl INTO wa_dtl  INDEX 1.
2482       ENDIF.
2483       IF wa_dtl-inst_type = 'LC'.
2484         MOVE 'BCONF'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2485         APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2486         MOVE 'SAVE'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2487         APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2488         MOVE 'PCONF'      TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2489         APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2490       ENDIF.
2491     ENDIF.
2492   *&---------<
2493     IF g_ans = 'N'.
2494       MOVE 'SAVE'        TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2495       APPEND wa_tab      TO  ist_tab      .
2496     ENDIF.
2497     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE status = 'B'.
2498       l_found = l_found + 1 .
2499     ENDLOOP.
2500     DESCRIBE TABLE ist_emddtl LINES l_lines.
2501     IF l_lines = l_found.
2502       MOVE 'SAVE'        TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2503       APPEND wa_tab      TO  ist_tab      .
2504       MOVE 'BCONF'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
2505       APPEND wa_tab    TO  ist_tab      .
2506     ENDIF.
2507   ENDFORM.                    " append_isttab_135

2576         g_error = 1.
2577       ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status NE 'B'.
2578         MESSAGE i497(zmm).
2579         g_ans = 1.
2580         g_error = 1.
2581
2582       ENDIF.
2583     ENDLOOP.
2584     IF l_found = 0.
2585       g_ans = 0.
2586     ELSE.
2587       g_ans = 1.
2588       MESSAGE i436(zmm).

2613       g_itemno = wa_emddtl-item_no.
2614
2615       IF wa_emddtl-status = 'N'.
2616         g_ans = 1.
2617         g_error = 1.
2618         MESSAGE i568(zmm).
2619       ENDIF.
2620   *+004
2621       IF NOT wa_emddtl-ri_reqno IS INITIAL.
2622         IF wa_emddtl-ri_reqno+14(1) = 'R'.
2623           IF NOT ( wa_emddtl-status = 'E' OR wa_emddtl-status = 'V' ).
2624             g_ans = 1.
2625             g_error = 1.
2626             MESSAGE i511(zmm).
2627             EXIT.
2628           ENDIF.
2629         ENDIF.
2630       ENDIF.
2631   *+004
2632       CASE wa_emddtl-inst_type.
2633
2634         WHEN 'BG'.
2635
2636           IF  wa_emddtl-status = 'D' .
2637             g_reset_status   = 'N' .
2638             EXIT.
2639           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'B' .   " send to bank
2640             g_reset_status   = 'N' .
2641             EXIT.
2642           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'A' OR wa_emddtl-status = 'Y'.
2643             g_reset_status   = 'B' .
2644             EXIT.
2645           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'S' .   "Submit to FI
2646             g_reset_status      = 'A' .
2647             EXIT.
2648           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'P' .   "Accepted by FI
2649             g_reset_status     = 'S' .
2650             EXIT.
2651           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'V' .    "Invoke
2652             g_reset_status     = 'P' .
2653             EXIT.
2654           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'E' .   "Return.
2655             g_reset_status     = 'B' .
2656             EXIT.
2657           ENDIF.
2658         WHEN 'LC'.
2659           IF  wa_emddtl-status = 'S' .   "Submit to FI
2660             g_reset_status      = 'N' .
2661             EXIT.
2662           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'P' .   "Accepted by FI
2663             g_reset_status     = 'S' .
2664             EXIT.
2665           ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'V' OR
2666                   wa_emddtl-status = 'E' .   "Invoke/Return.
2667             g_reset_status     = 'P' .
2668             EXIT.
2669           ENDIF.
2670       ENDCASE.
2671     ENDLOOP.
2672
2673     IF l_found = 0.
2674       g_ans = 0.
2675     ELSE.
2676       g_ans = 1.
2677       MESSAGE i438(zmm).
2678     ENDIF.
2679   ENDFORM.                    " check_sel_reset_item
2680   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2681   *&      Form  update_post_conf
2682   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2683   *       Update Status when Post Confirmation
2684   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2685   *  -->  p1        text
2686   *  <--  p2        text
2687   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2688   FORM update_post_conf USING p_ad.
2689     DATA l_status .
2690     IF p_ad = 1.
2691       l_status = 'A'.  "Accepted By the bank
2692     ELSEIF p_ad = 2.
2693       l_status = 'Y'.  "Denied  By the  Bnak
2694     ELSE.
2695     ENDIF.
2696   *&----> Flag 'A' is set for post Confirmation accepted
2697   *----  other wise 'Y' for deny
2698     LOOP AT  ist_emddtl  INTO wa_emddtl WHERE status = 'B' AND
2699              sel = 'X'.
2700
2701       UPDATE zmm_emddtl
2702              SET status = l_status
2703        WHERE docno      = wa_emdhdr-docno   AND
2704              trans      = wa_emdhdr-trans   AND
2705              item_no    = wa_emddtl-item_no AND
2706              status     = 'B'.
2707     ENDLOOP.
2708   ENDFORM.                    " update_post_conf
2709   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2710   *&      Form  get_item_details
2711   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
2712   *      Get Item Details
2713   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2714   *  -->  p1        text
2715   *  <--  p2        text
2716   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2717   FORM get_item_details.
2718     DATA l_found .
2719     CLEAR g_status.
2720     CLEAR g_reqno .
2721     CLEAR g_reqtext .
2722     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X'.
2723       l_found = 'X'.
2724       CASE wa_emddtl-status .
2725         WHEN 'N'.
2726           g_status        = text-040.  "Receipt
2727         WHEN 'B'.
2728           g_status        = text-036.  "Send for Bank Confirmation' .
2729         WHEN 'A'.
2730           g_status        = text-037 . "'Acceped By Bank'.
2731         WHEN  'Y'.
2732           g_status        = text-038 . " 'Denied  By Bank'.
2733         WHEN  'V'.
2734           g_status       =  text-039 . "'Invoked'.
2735         WHEN 'S'.
2736           g_status       =  text-043 .
2737         WHEN 'I'.
2738           g_status       =  text-054 .
2739         WHEN 'D'.
2740           g_status       =  text-047 .
2741         WHEN 'E'.
2742           g_status       =  text-058 .
2743         WHEN 'P'.
2744           g_status      = text-061.
2745       ENDCASE.
2746   *+003
2747       IF wa_emddtl-ri_stat = '1'.
2748         g_reqtext   = text-058.
2749       ELSEIF wa_emddtl-ri_stat = '2'.
2750         g_reqtext   = text-078.
2751       ELSEIF wa_emddtl-ri_stat = '3'.
2752         g_reqtext  = text-080.
2753       ELSE.
2754         g_reqtext = 'Nill'.
2755       ENDIF.
2756     ENDLOOP.
2757
2758     IF NOT wa_emddtl-ri_reqno IS INITIAL. "AND wa_emddtl-ri_stat NE '3' .
2759       g_reqno = wa_emddtl-ri_reqno.
2760     ELSE.
2761       g_reqno = 'Nill'.
2762     ENDIF.
2763   *+003
2764
2765     MOVE wa_emddtl-amount  TO zmm_emddtl-amount.
2766     IF l_found = 'X'.
2767       CALL SCREEN 0140 STARTING AT 10 5  ENDING AT 65 15 .
2768     ELSE.
2769       MESSAGE i442(zmm).
2770     ENDIF.
2771   ENDFORM.                    " get_item_details
2772   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

2778   *  <--  p2        text
2779   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
2780   FORM get_bglc_data.
2781     REFRESH  ist_emddtl    .
2782     CLEAR:   ist_emddtl    ,
2783              wa_emdhdr     ,
2784              wa_emddtl01   ,
2785              g_status      ,
2786              g_reset       ,
2787              g_dansw       ,
2788              g_reset       .
2789
2790
2791     IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-docno IS INITIAL.
2792       SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
2793    WHERE DOCNO = G_IDOCNO
2794    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
2795    ENDSELECT.
2796       IF sy-subrc = 0.
2797   *&--> Display Status using variable g_status.
2798         IF wa_emdhdr-status = 'N'.
2799           g_status  = 'Created'.
2800         ELSEIF wa_emdhdr-status = 'D'.
2801           IF prev_okcode = 'DELE'.
2802             MESSAGE e425(zmm).
2803           ELSE.
2804             g_status  = 'Deleted'.
2805           ENDIF.
2806         ELSE.
2807           g_reset   = 0.
2808         ENDIF.

2810         IF wa_emdhdr-status = 'D'.
2811           MESSAGE w416(zmm) WITH  wa_emdhdr-docno.
2812   *       perform popup_for_reset  .
2813           g_reset = 1.
2814         ENDIF.
2815
2816   *&-----> Get Place NAme from table zmm_emdloc  .
2817
2818         SELECT SINGLE locname FROM zmm_emdloc INTO g_locname
2819                WHERE locid = wa_emdhdr-place .
2820   *&-----<
2821         MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emdhdr TO zmm_emdhdr .
2822         SELECT * FROM zmm_emddtl INTO TABLE ist_emddtl01
2823                  WHERE docno = wa_emdhdr-docno  AND
2824                        trans = wa_emdhdr-trans  AND
2825                        inst_type IN ('BG','LC') ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
2826         IF sy-subrc = 0.
2827   *&--> Append EMD Item data into table control internal table ist_emddtl.
2828   *Start of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009
2829           CLEAR wa_emddtl.
2830   *End of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009
2831           LOOP AT ist_emddtl01 INTO wa_emddtl01.
2832             MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emddtl01 TO wa_emddtl.
2833             APPEND wa_emddtl TO ist_emddtl.
2834   *Start of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009
2835             CLEAR wa_emddtl01.
2836             CLEAR wa_emddtl.
2837   *End of addition by SAB_SARVANAN on 12/06/2009
2838           ENDLOOP.
2839   *&--<
2840         ELSE.
2841           MESSAGE e411(zmm).
2842         ENDIF.
2843       ELSE.
2844         MESSAGE e411(zmm).
2845       ENDIF.
2846     ENDIF.
2847   ENDFORM.                    " get_bglc_data

3241       ENDLOOP.
3242     ELSE.
3243       LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X'.
3244
3245         IF  wa_emddtl-status = 'E'.
3246           MESSAGE i452(zmm).
3247           g_ans = 1.
3248           g_error = 1.
3249         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'V' .
3250           MESSAGE i443(zmm).
3251           g_ans = 1.
3252           g_error = 1.
3253         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'N'.
3254           g_ans = 1.
3255           g_error = 1.
3256           MESSAGE i497(zmm).
3257         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'B'.
3258           g_ans = 1.
3259           g_error = 1.
3260           MESSAGE i447(zmm).
3261         ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'Y'   AND g_mmret NE 'X' .
3262   *+004
3263           AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'ZMMBGLC'
3264                ID 'ACTVT' FIELD  '16'   .
3265           IF sy-subrc = 0 .
3266             MESSAGE e515(zmm).
3267           ELSE.
3268             MESSAGE e505(zmm).
3269           ENDIF.
3270         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'A'  AND g_mmret NE 'X' .
3271   *+004
3272           AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'ZMMBGLC'
3273                ID 'ACTVT' FIELD  '16'   .
3274           IF sy-subrc = 0 .
3275             MESSAGE e516(zmm).
3276           ELSE.
3277             MESSAGE e505(zmm).
3278           ENDIF.
3279   *+004
3280   *-004
3281   *        g_ans = 1.
3282   *        g_error = 1.
3283   *        message i498(zmm).
3284   *
3285         ENDIF.
3286   * +003
3287         IF wa_emddtl-ri_stat = '2'.
3288           g_ans = 1.
3289           g_error = 1.
3290           MESSAGE  e587(zmm).
3291         ELSEIF  ( wa_emddtl-ri_stat = space OR wa_emddtl-ri_stat = '3' ) AND
3292                                  g_mmret NE 'X'  .
3293           g_ans = 1.
3294           g_error = 1.
3295           MESSAGE  e588(zmm).
3296         ENDIF.
3297
3298   *
3299   *      if  wa_emddtl-ri_reqno+14(1) ne  'R' AND g_MMRET NE 'X' .
3300   *        g_ans = 1.
3301   *        g_error = 1.
3302   *        message  i588(zmm).
3303   *      endif.
3304   * +003
3305
3306
3307   *+004
3308   *      if p_stat ne 'X'.
3309   *        if wa_emddtl-status = 'S'.
3310   *          g_ans = 1.
3311   *          g_error = 1.
3312   *          message i496(zmm).
3313   *        elseif wa_emddtl-status = 'A'.
3314   *          g_ans = 1.
3315   *          g_error = 1.
3316   *          message i450(zmm).
3317   *        endif.
3318   *+004
3319   *     endif.
3320       ENDLOOP.
3321     ENDIF.
3322   ENDFORM.                    " check_sel_item_RETN
3323   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3324   *&      Form  retrun_docu
3325   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3326   *     Update status of return Document
3327   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3328   *  -->  p1        text
3329   *  <--  p2        text
3330   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3331   FORM retrun_docu.
3332
3333   *&----> Flag 'E' is set for post Confirmation
3334     LOOP AT  ist_emddtl  INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X' .
3335       UPDATE zmm_emddtl
3336              SET status = 'E' ridate = sy-datum riuname = sy-uname
3337        WHERE docno      = wa_emdhdr-docno   AND
3338              trans      = wa_emdhdr-trans   AND
3339              item_no    = wa_emddtl-item_no AND
3340              status    IN ('P','Y','A').
3341     ENDLOOP.
3342   ENDFORM.                    " retrun_docu
3343   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3344   *&      Form  MODIFY_DATA_S145
3345   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3346   *     Save Data of screen 145
3347   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3348   *  -->  p1        text
3349   *  <--  p2        text
3350   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3351   FORM modify_data_s145.
3352
3353     CLEAR   : ist_dtl, ist_scrdtl .
3354     REFRESH : ist_scrdtl .
3355     REFRESH  ist_emddtl01.
3356     CLEAR:   wa_emddtl,
3357              wa_emddtl01.
3358     CLEAR    g_amount.
3359     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl .
3360       g_amount  = g_amount + wa_emddtl-amount    .
3361
3362       MOVE-CORRESPONDING wa_emddtl  TO    wa_emddtl01           .
3363       MOVE wa_emddtl-status         TO    wa_emddtl01-status    .
3364       MOVE g_docno                  TO    wa_emddtl01-docno     .
3365       MOVE zmm_emdhdr-trans         TO    wa_emddtl01-trans     .
3366       MOVE zmm_emdhdr-ebeln         TO    wa_emddtl01-ebeln     .
3367       MOVE zmm_emdhdr-vendorno      TO    wa_emddtl01-vendno    .
3368       MOVE zmm_emdhdr-tenderno      TO    wa_emddtl01-tendno    .
3369       MOVE wa_emdhdr-bstyp          TO    wa_emddtl01-bstyp      .
3370       MOVE space                    TO   wa_emddtl01-fi_parkno  .
3371       wa_emddtl01-crby = sy-uname .
3372       wa_emddtl01-cron = sy-datum .
3373       MOVE g_idocno             TO  wa_emddtl01-docno  .
3374       APPEND  wa_emddtl01 TO ist_emddtl01 .
3375     ENDLOOP.
3376     IF NOT g_amount IS INITIAL.
3377       UPDATE zmm_emdhdr
3378            SET amount  = g_amount
3379            WHERE trans = zmm_emdhdr-trans  AND
3380                  docno = g_idocno.
3381       IF sy-subrc = 0.
3382         g_save_h = 0.
3383       ELSE.
3384         g_save_h = 1.
3385       ENDIF.
3386     ENDIF.
3387
3388     IF NOT ist_emddtl01 IS INITIAL.
3389
3390       MODIFY zmm_emddtl FROM TABLE ist_emddtl01.
3391       IF sy-subrc = 0.
3392         g_save_i = 0.
3393       ELSE.
3394         g_save_i = 1.
3395       ENDIF.
3396     ENDIF.
3397   * Update CDPOS and CDHDR table - Change document for fields
3398   * RSCODE, AMOUNT and INST_VDT (table ZMM_EMDDTL).
3399
3400     ist_scrdtl[] = ist_emddtl[] ." screen fields
3401     LOOP AT ist_scrdtl .
3402
3403       READ TABLE ist_dtl WITH KEY docno   = ist_scrdtl-docno
3404                                   item_no = ist_scrdtl-item_no.
3405
3406       IF ist_dtl-rscode <> ist_scrdtl-rscode .
3407         PERFORM chg_docu_rscode .
3408       ENDIF .
3409       READ TABLE ist_dtl WITH KEY docno = ist_scrdtl-docno
3410                                   item_no = ist_scrdtl-item_no.
3411
3412       IF ist_dtl-amount <> ist_scrdtl-amount .
3413         PERFORM chg_docu_amount .
3414       ENDIF .
3415       READ TABLE ist_dtl WITH KEY docno = ist_scrdtl-docno
3416                                 item_no = ist_scrdtl-item_no.
3417
3418       IF ist_dtl-inst_vdt <> ist_scrdtl-inst_vdt .
3419         PERFORM chg_docu_validity .
3420       ENDIF .
3421     ENDLOOP .
3422   ENDFORM.                    " MODIFY_DATA_S145
3423   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3424   *&      Form  CHECK_BGLC_DOC
3425   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3426   *       Cehck BG/LC Document
3427   *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3428   *  -->  p1        text
3429   *  <--  p2        text
3430   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3431   FORM check_bglc_doc.
3432
3433     SELECT SINGLE  * FROM zmm_emddtl
3434            WHERE docno  = zmm_emdhdr-docno AND
3435                    inst_type IN ('BG','LC').
3436
3437     IF sy-subrc = 0.
3438     ELSE.
3439       MESSAGE e461(zmm).
3440     ENDIF.
3441   ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_BGLC_DOC
3442   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3443   *&      Form  check_valid_docno
3444   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3445   *       text
3446   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3447   *  -->  p1        text
3448   *  <--  p2        text
3449   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3450   FORM check_valid_docno.
3451     DATA: l_inst_type LIKE zmm_emddtl-inst_type .
3452     CLEAR wa_emdhdr.
3453     CLEAR g_ebeln  .
3454
3455     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
3456    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
3457    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3458    ENDSELECT.
3459
3460     IF sy-subrc = 0.
3461       CLEAR g_ebeln  .
3462   *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3463   *  If instrument BG/LC exist in Doc. then don't allow for*
3464   *  Refund/forfeit/Conversion.
3465       SELECT INST_TYPE FROM ZMM_EMDDTL INTO L_INST_TYPE UP TO 1 ROWS
3466    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
3467    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3468    ENDSELECT.
3469
3470       IF sy-subrc = 0.
3471         IF l_inst_type = 'BG' OR l_inst_type = 'LC'.
3472           MESSAGE e560(zmm).
3473         ENDIF.
3474       ENDIF.
3475   *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3476   *&-- Check whether transaction is Tender Fee.
3477   *& If tender fee no Forfeit Allowed.
3478       IF g_doccat = text-016 AND wa_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
3479         MESSAGE e555(zmm).
3480       ENDIF.
3481
3482       IF g_doccat = 'EMD-SD CNV' AND wa_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
3483         MESSAGE e558(zmm).
3484       ENDIF.
3485
3486       IF g_doccat = 'EMD-SD CNV' AND wa_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
3487         MESSAGE e559(zmm).
3488       ENDIF.
3489       MOVE wa_emdhdr TO zmm_emdhdr.
3490   *Deleted
3491       IF wa_emdhdr-status = 'D'.
3492         MESSAGE e425(zmm).
3493   * Partially Parked
3494       ELSEIF  wa_emdhdr-status = 'K'.
3495         MESSAGE e462(zmm).
3496   *   Fully Parked
3497       ELSEIF wa_emdhdr-status = 'U'.
3498       ELSEIF wa_emdhdr-status = 'N'.
3499         MESSAGE e478(zmm).
3500       ENDIF.
3501     ELSE.
3502       SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF INTO WA_EMDREF UP TO 1 ROWS
3503    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
3504    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3505    ENDSELECT.
3506       IF sy-subrc = 0.
3507         CLEAR g_ebeln .
3508   *------ For EMD- SD converted documents further conversion is not *  *
3509   * possible.
3510
3511         IF wa_emdref-rfcstat = 'C' AND  g_doccat = 'EMD-SD CNV' .
3512           MESSAGE e564(zmm).
3513         ENDIF.
3514
3515   *--------<
3516   *-------> Raise error if document status is 'R' OR 'F' in create mode.
3517         IF ok_code = 'CRET'.
3518           IF  wa_emdref-rfcstat NE  'C'.
3519             MESSAGE e565(zmm).
3520           ENDIF.
3521         ENDIF.
3522   *-------<
3523
3524         IF wa_emdref-status = 'D'.
3525           MESSAGE e425(zmm).
3526         ELSEIF  wa_emdref-status NE 'S'.
3527           MESSAGE e450(zmm).
3528         ENDIF.
3529   *---- IF valid docno then select header details from table zmm_emdhdr
3530   * based on refdocno.
3531         SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_emdhdr INTO wa_emdhdr
3532              WHERE docno  = wa_emdref-refdoc .
3533
3534         MOVE wa_emdref-amount TO zmm_emdhdr-amount.
3535         MOVE wa_emdref-currency TO zmm_emdref-currency.
3536         MOVE wa_emdref-ebeln TO g_ebeln  .
3537       ELSE.
3538         MESSAGE e411(zmm).
3539       ENDIF.
3540     ENDIF.
3541   ENDFORM.                    " check_valid_docno
3542
3543   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3544   *&      Form  check_reason_code

3549   *  <--  p2        text
3550   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3551   FORM check_reason_code USING p_rscode.
3552

3556     IF sy-subrc NE 0.
3557       MESSAGE e467(zmm).
3558     ENDIF.

3561       IF wa_tc105-rscode < 100 OR wa_tc105-rscode > 199 .
3562         MESSAGE e464(zmm).
3563       ENDIF.
3564     ELSEIF  zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD' .
3565       IF wa_tc105-rscode < 200 OR wa_tc105-rscode > 299  .
3566         MESSAGE e465(zmm).
3567       ENDIF.
3568     ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
3569       IF wa_tc105-rscode <  300  OR wa_tc105-rscode >  399   .
3570         MESSAGE e466(zmm).
3571       ENDIF.
3572   *-------  < Check Reason code > ---------------------------------------*
3573     ENDIF.

3614   *&      Form  DEL_LINE_ITEM
3615   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3616   *      Delete Line Item from table ZMM_EMDDTL USING Internal table
3617   *      ist_del_emddtl.

3633   *  from table zmm_emddtl.

3636                       docno   = wa_emddtl-docno   .

3642   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3643   *&      Form  check_rfc_amount_receipt
3644   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3645   *       Check Balance amount at the time of creation. Refund/Forfeit/
3646   *        EMD-SD Convert
3647   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3648   *  -->  p1        text
3649   *  <--  p2        text
3650   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3651   FORM check_rfc_amount_receipt.
3652
3653   *&---> Check Refund Amount
3654     PERFORM get_total_ref  .
3655   *&---<
3656     IF   g_tot_ref IS INITIAL.
3657       g_balamt = zmm_emdhdr-amount .
3658     ELSE.
3659       g_balamt = zmm_emdhdr-amount - g_tot_ref.
3660     ENDIF.
3661
3662     IF zmm_emdref-amount <  g_balamt AND NOT zmm_emdref-amount IS INITIAL.
3663       g_balamt  =  g_balamt - zmm_emdref-amount   .
3664     ELSEIF  zmm_emdref-amount > g_balamt .
3665       MESSAGE e429(zmm) WITH g_doccat .
3666     ELSEIF   zmm_emdref-amount IS INITIAL.
3667       g_balamt  = zmm_emdhdr-amount - g_tot_ref.
3668     ELSEIF zmm_emdref-amount =  g_balamt AND NOT zmm_emdref-amount IS
3669        INITIAL.
3670       g_balamt  =  g_balamt - zmm_emdref-amount   .
3671     ENDIF.
3672
3673   ENDFORM.                    " check_rfc_amount_receipt
3674   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3675   *&      Form  get_head_status
3676   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3677   *      Get Header Status using g_status
3678   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3679   *      -->P_G_STAT  text
3680   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3681   FORM get_head_status USING    p_stat.
3682
3683     CLEAR :
3684             g_status  ,
3685             g_hstatus .
3686     IF  p_stat    = 'N'.
3687       g_status    = text-040.
3688       g_hstatus   = text-040.
3689     ELSEIF p_stat = 'U'.
3690       g_status    = text-049.
3691       g_hstatus   = text-049.
3692     ELSEIF p_stat = 'D'.
3693       g_status    = text-047.
3694       g_hstatus   = text-047.
3695     ELSEIF p_stat = 'K'.
3696       g_status    = text-055.
3697       g_hstatus   = text-055.
3698     ELSEIF p_stat = 'X'.
3699       g_status    = text-057.
3700       g_hstatus   = text-057.
3701     ELSEIF p_stat = 'J'.
3702       g_status    = text-062.
3703       g_hstatus   = text-062.
3704     ELSEIF p_stat = 'Q'.
3705       g_status    = text-063.
3706       g_hstatus   = text-063.
3707     ELSEIF p_stat = 'O'.
3708       g_status    = text-065.
3709       g_hstatus   = text-065.
3710     ELSEIF p_stat = 'T'.
3711       g_status    = text-066.
3712       g_hstatus   = text-066.
3713     ELSEIF p_stat = 'X'.
3714       g_status    = text-067.
3715       g_hstatus   = text-067.
3716   * Begin of <RD1K963111>
3717     ELSEIF p_stat = 'R'.
3718       g_status    = text-015.
3719       g_hstatus   = text-015.
3720     ELSEIF p_stat = 'I'.
3721       g_status    = text-054.
3722       g_hstatus   = text-054.
3723     ELSEIF p_stat = 'G'.
3724       g_status    = text-083.
3725       g_hstatus   = text-083.
3726   * End of <RD1K963111>
3727   *+007 : Status
3728     ELSEIF p_stat = '0'.
3729
3730       PERFORM get_status USING    p_stat
3731                          CHANGING g_status.
3732
3733       MOVE g_status TO g_hstatus.
3734   *+007 : End
3735
3736     ENDIF.
3737   ENDFORM.                    " get_head_status
3738   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3739   *&      Form  get_item_status
3740   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3741   *      Get Item status.
3742   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3743   *      -->P_WA_EMDDTL_STATUS  text
3744   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3745   FORM get_item_status USING    p_status.
3746
3747     CLEAR g_status.
3748     CASE p_status .
3749       WHEN 'N'.
3750         g_status        = text-040.  "Receipt
3751       WHEN 'B'.
3752         g_status        = text-036.  "Send for Bank Confirmation' .
3753       WHEN 'A'.
3754         g_status        = text-037 . "'Acceped By Bank'.
3755       WHEN  'Y'.
3756         g_status        = text-038 . " 'Denied  By Bank'.
3757       WHEN  'V'.
3758         g_status       =  text-039 . "'Invoked'.
3759       WHEN 'S'.
3760         g_status       =  text-043 .  "Submitted to FI
3761       WHEN 'I'.
3762         g_status       =  text-054 .  "Parked to FI
3763       WHEN 'D'.
3764         g_status       =  text-047 .   "Deleted
3765       WHEN 'R'.
3766         g_status       =  text-015.    "Refund
3767       WHEN 'F'.
3768         g_status       =  text-016.    "Forfeit
3769       WHEN 'C'.
3770         g_status       =  text-017.    "Convert
3771       WHEN 'P'.
3772         g_status      =  text-061.    "Accepted by FI
3773       WHEN 'E'.
3774         g_status      =  text-058.    "Return BG/LC Document.
3775     ENDCASE.
3776
3777   ENDFORM.                    " get_item_status
3778   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3779   *&      Form  get_ref_doc_stat

3791

3794     ENDIF.
3795   ENDFORM.                    " get_ref_doc_stat

3839   *  -->  p1        text
3840   *  <--  p2        text
3841   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3842   FORM update_head_status_105.
3843     DATA: l_prev_stat.
3844     DATA: l_found .
3845     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl.
3846       IF sy-tabix = 1.
3847         l_prev_stat = wa_emddtl-status.
3848       ENDIF.
3849       IF wa_emddtl-status NE l_prev_stat.
3850         l_found = 'X'.
3851       ELSE.
3852         l_found = space.
3853       ENDIF.
3854     ENDLOOP.
3855   ENDFORM.                    " update_head_status_105
3856   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3857   *&      Form  UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS
3858   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3859   *     Update Status at Header Level.
3860   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3861   *  -->  p1        text
3862   *  <--  p2        text
3863   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3864   FORM update_header_status  .
3865   *using p_docno.
3866     DATA: wa_emddtl_tmp LIKE wa_emddtl.
3867     DATA: l_h_stat.
3868     DATA: l_found .
3869     CLEAR g_h_status.
3870   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3871   * Before Save Update Header Status based on the the document status at
3872   * Item level.
3873   * Eg. If All line items are parked then update Header status as Fully *
3874   * parked.
3875   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3876
3877     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl_tmp.
3878       IF sy-tabix = 1.
3879         l_h_stat = wa_emddtl_tmp-status.
3880       ENDIF.
3881
3882       IF l_h_stat NE wa_emddtl_tmp-status.
3883         l_found = 'X'.
3884         EXIT.
3885       ELSE.
3886         l_found = '0'.
3887       ENDIF.
3888     ENDLOOP.
3889
3890     IF l_found NE 'X'.
3891       IF l_h_stat = 'I'.
3892         g_h_status = 'U'.
3893       ELSEIF l_h_stat = 'N'.
3894         g_h_status = 'N'.
3895       ENDIF.
3896     ELSE.
3897     ENDIF.
3898   ENDFORM.                    " UPDATE_HEADER_STATUS
3899   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3900   *&      Form  check_rfc_docu_status
3901   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3902   *      Check Refund/Forfeit/Convert Document Status
3903   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3904   *  -->  p1        text
3905   *  <--  p2        text
3906   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3907   FORM check_rfc_docu_status.
3908   *--------- Check ZMM_EMDREF ----------------------------*
3909     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF INTO WA_EMDREF UP TO 1 ROWS
3910    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO
3911    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3912    ENDSELECT.
3913
3914     IF prev_okcode = 'DELE'.
3915       IF wa_emdref-status = 'R' OR
3916          wa_emdref-status =  'F'.
3917       ELSE.
3918         IF wa_emdref-status = 'D'.
3919           MESSAGE e425(zmm).
3920         ELSEIF wa_emdref-status = 'I'.
3921           MESSAGE e482(zmm)   .
3922         ENDIF.
3923       ENDIF.
3924     ELSEIF prev_okcode = 'UNDEL'.
3925       IF wa_emdref-status =  'R' OR
3926        wa_emdref-status   =  'F' OR
3927        wa_emdref-status   = 'I'.
3928         MESSAGE e483(zmm).
3929       ELSE.
3930       ENDIF.
3931     ENDIF.
3932   ENDFORM.                    " check_rfc_docu_status
3933   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3934   *&      Form  CHECK_AMOUNT_BF_RESET
3935   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3936   *      Check Balance amount before Reset.
3937   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3938   *  -->  p1        text
3939   *  <--  p2        text
3940   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3941   FORM check_amount_bf_reset.
3942     PERFORM get_total_rfc_amt  .
3943   ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_AMOUNT_BF_RESET
3944   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3945   *&      Form  get_total_rfc_amt
3946   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3947   *    This subroutine is being used for  getting tot Refund/Forfeit/
3948   *    EMD-SD Conv. at the time of RESET
3949   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3950   *  -->  p1        text
3951   *  <--  p2        text
3952   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3953   FORM get_total_rfc_amt .
3954     DATA: wa_emdref_temp  LIKE zmm_emdref.
3955     DATA: wa_emdref_t001  LIKE zmm_emdref.
3956     DATA: l_h_amt LIKE    zmm_emdhdr-amount.
3957     DATA: l_bal_amt       LIKE zmm_emdhdr-amount .
3958     CLEAR l_h_amt .
3959     CLEAR : wa_emdref_temp ,
3960             wa_emdref_t001 .
3961   *---------------------------------------------------------------------*
3962   *  Get Ref docno.
3963     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF INTO WA_EMDREF_TEMP UP TO 1 ROWS
3964    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO
3965    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3966    ENDSELECT.
3967
3968   * Check total amount at header level.
3969     IF sy-subrc = 0.
3970       SELECT AMOUNT INTO L_H_AMT FROM ZMM_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
3971    WHERE DOCNO = WA_EMDREF_TEMP-REFDOC
3972    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3973    ENDSELECT.
3974
3975   *-- Check in case of converted Document. search in table ZMM_EMDREF.
3976       IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
3977         SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDREF INTO WA_EMDREF_T001 UP TO 1 ROWS
3978    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDREF-DOCNO
3979    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3980    ENDSELECT.
3981         IF sy-subrc = 0.
3982
3983           SELECT AMOUNT INTO L_H_AMT FROM ZMM_EMDREF UP TO 1 ROWS
3984    WHERE DOCNO = WA_EMDREF_T001-REFDOC AND RFCSTAT = 'C'
3985    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
3986    ENDSELECT.
3987
3988         ENDIF.
3989       ENDIF.
3990     ENDIF.
3991   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
3992     g_tot_ref  =  0.
3993     CLEAR g_tot_ref .
3994
3995     SELECT * FROM zmm_emdref INTO TABLE ist_emdref
3996                WHERE     refdoc  = wa_emdref_temp-refdoc AND
3997                          status IN ('R','F','C','I','S').
3998
3999     IF sy-subrc = 0.
4000       LOOP AT ist_emdref INTO wa_emdref.
4001         g_tot_ref = g_tot_ref + wa_emdref-amount.
4002       ENDLOOP.
4003     ENDIF.
4004   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4005   * Check whether the RFC amount is greater than or equal to Header amt
4006   * then don't allow to RESET Document .
4007
4008     l_bal_amt  =  l_h_amt - g_tot_ref .
4009
4010     IF NOT g_tot_ref IS  INITIAL.
4011       IF  wa_emdref_temp-amount  > l_bal_amt.
4012         MESSAGE e484(zmm) WITH  wa_emdref_temp-amount  .
4013       ENDIF.
4014     ENDIF.
4015   ENDFORM.                    " get_total_rfc_amt

4022   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*

4033     ENDIF.
4034   ENDFORM.                    " check_EMDCONV_DOC

4082   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4083   *&      Form  cehck_sel_item_accept
4084   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4085   *       Check selected item before Accept by Finance
4086   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4087   *  -->  p1        text
4088   *  <--  p2        text
4089   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4090   FORM cehck_sel_item_accept.
4091     DATA l_found .
4092     l_found = 1.
4093     g_error = 0.
4094
4095   *&---> Check whether any line Item  selected or Not
4096
4097     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X'.
4098       l_found = 0.
4099       EXIT.
4100     ENDLOOP.
4101   *&----<
4102     IF l_found = 0.
4103       g_ans = 0.
4104     ELSE.
4105       g_ans = 1.
4106       MESSAGE i493(zmm).
4107     ENDIF.
4108
4109     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X'.
4110       IF  wa_emddtl-status = 'E'.
4111         MESSAGE i452(zmm).
4112         g_ans = 1.
4113         g_error = 1.
4114       ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'V' .
4115         MESSAGE i443(zmm).
4116         g_ans = 1.
4117         g_error = 1.
4118       ELSEIF  wa_emddtl-status = 'P'.
4119         MESSAGE i495(zmm).
4120         g_ans = 1.
4121         g_error = 1.
4122
4123       ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status NE 'S' .
4124         MESSAGE i450(zmm).
4125         g_ans   = 1.
4126         g_error = 1.
4127       ELSE.
4128         g_ans   = 0.
4129         g_error = 0.
4130
4131       ENDIF.
4132     ENDLOOP.
4133   ENDFORM.                    " cehck_sel_item_accept
4134   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4135   *&      Form  accept_docu
4136   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4137   *    Accept Document by
4138   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4139   *  -->  p1        text
4140   *  <--  p2        text
4141   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4142   FORM accept_docu.
4143
4144   *&----> Flag 'P' is set FOR Accept by FI.
4145   *------- Before this updation the status of the line item must be 'S' ie
4146   * Submitted by FI.
4147
4148     LOOP AT  ist_emddtl  INTO wa_emddtl WHERE sel = 'X' .
4149
4150       UPDATE zmm_emddtl
4151              SET status = 'P'
4152        WHERE docno      = wa_emdhdr-docno   AND
4153              trans      = wa_emdhdr-trans   AND
4154              item_no    = wa_emddtl-item_no AND
4155              status     = 'S'.
4156     ENDLOOP.
4157
4158     IF sy-subrc = 0.
4159       MESSAGE s494(zmm).
4160     ENDIF.
4161   ENDFORM.                    " accept_docu
4162   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4163   *&      Form  disp_chg_hstry
4164   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4165   *       Sub routine being used to display change history for the fields
4166   *       RSCODE, AMOUNT, INST_VDT.
4167   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4168   *  -->  p1        text
4169   *  <--  p2        text
4170   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4171   FORM disp_chg_hstry.
4172     DATA :   i_cdhdr TYPE TABLE OF cdhdr WITH HEADER LINE  .
4173     DATA : ist_cdhdr TYPE TABLE OF cdhdr WITH HEADER LINE .
4174     DATA : l_objid LIKE cdhdr-objectid .
4175     DATA : BEGIN  OF ist_editpos OCCURS 0 .
4176             INCLUDE STRUCTURE cdshw .
4177     DATA :  user LIKE sy-uname,
4178             date LIKE sy-datum,
4179             END OF ist_editpos .
4180     DATA :   i_editpos TYPE TABLE OF cdshw WITH HEADER LINE .
4181     DATA : wa_header LIKE cdhdr .
4182     l_objid = wa_emddtl-docno .
4183
4184     CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_HEADERS'
4185       EXPORTING
4186         objectclass                = 'ZRSCODE'
4187         objectid                   = l_objid
4188       TABLES
4189         i_cdhdr                    = i_cdhdr
4190       EXCEPTIONS
4191         no_position_found          = 1
4192         wrong_access_to_archive    = 2
4193         time_zone_conversion_error = 3
4194         OTHERS                     = 4.
4195     IF sy-subrc =  0.
4196       CLEAR : i_cdhdr .
4197       LOOP AT i_cdhdr .
4198         CLEAR : i_editpos, ist_editpos , wa_header .
4199         REFRESH i_editpos .
4200         CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_POSITIONS'
4201           EXPORTING
4202             changenumber            = i_cdhdr-changenr
4203           IMPORTING
4204             header                  = wa_header
4205           TABLES
4206             editpos                 = i_editpos
4207           EXCEPTIONS
4208             no_position_found       = 1
4209             wrong_access_to_archive = 2
4210             OTHERS                  = 3.
4211         IF sy-subrc =  0.
4212           LOOP AT i_editpos WHERE fname = 'RSCODE' .
4213             MOVE-CORRESPONDING i_editpos TO ist_editpos.
4214             ist_editpos-user = wa_header-username .
4215             ist_editpos-date = wa_header-udate .
4216             APPEND ist_editpos .
4217           ENDLOOP.
4218         ENDIF.
4219       ENDLOOP .
4220     ENDIF .
4221     REFRESH i_cdhdr .
4222     CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_HEADERS'
4223       EXPORTING
4224         objectclass                = 'ZAMOUNT'
4225         objectid                   = l_objid
4226       TABLES
4227         i_cdhdr                    = i_cdhdr
4228       EXCEPTIONS
4229         no_position_found          = 1
4230         wrong_access_to_archive    = 2
4231         time_zone_conversion_error = 3
4232         OTHERS                     = 4.
4233     IF sy-subrc =  0.
4234
4235       CLEAR : i_cdhdr, i_editpos  .
4236       REFRESH : i_editpos .
4237       LOOP AT i_cdhdr .
4238         CLEAR : i_editpos, ist_editpos , wa_header .
4239         CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_POSITIONS'
4240           EXPORTING
4241             changenumber            = i_cdhdr-changenr
4242           IMPORTING
4243             header                  = wa_header
4244           TABLES
4245             editpos                 = i_editpos
4246           EXCEPTIONS
4247             no_position_found       = 1
4248             wrong_access_to_archive = 2
4249             OTHERS                  = 3.
4250         IF sy-subrc =  0.
4251           LOOP AT i_editpos WHERE fname = 'AMOUNT' .
4252             MOVE-CORRESPONDING i_editpos TO ist_editpos.
4253             ist_editpos-user = wa_header-username .
4254             ist_editpos-date = wa_header-udate .
4255             APPEND ist_editpos .
4256           ENDLOOP.
4257         ENDIF.
4258       ENDLOOP .
4259     ENDIF .
4260
4261     REFRESH i_cdhdr .
4262     CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_HEADERS'
4263       EXPORTING
4264         objectclass                = 'ZVALIDITY'
4265         objectid                   = l_objid
4266       TABLES
4267         i_cdhdr                    = i_cdhdr
4268       EXCEPTIONS
4269         no_position_found          = 1
4270         wrong_access_to_archive    = 2
4271         time_zone_conversion_error = 3
4272         OTHERS                     = 4.
4273     IF sy-subrc =  0.
4274
4275       CLEAR : i_cdhdr, i_editpos  .
4276       REFRESH i_editpos .
4277       LOOP AT i_cdhdr .
4278         CLEAR : i_editpos, ist_editpos , wa_header .
4279         CALL FUNCTION 'CHANGEDOCUMENT_READ_POSITIONS'
4280           EXPORTING
4281             changenumber            = i_cdhdr-changenr
4282           IMPORTING
4283             header                  = wa_header
4284           TABLES
4285             editpos                 = i_editpos
4286           EXCEPTIONS
4287             no_position_found       = 1
4288             wrong_access_to_archive = 2
4289             OTHERS                  = 3.
4290         IF sy-subrc =  0.
4291           LOOP AT i_editpos WHERE fname = 'INST_VDT' .
4292             MOVE-CORRESPONDING i_editpos TO ist_editpos.
4293             ist_editpos-user = wa_header-username .
4294             ist_editpos-date = wa_header-udate .
4295             APPEND ist_editpos .
4296           ENDLOOP.
4297         ENDIF.
4298       ENDLOOP .
4299     ENDIF .
4300
4301     WRITE : /5 'Document No. ' , 20 'Field Name', 35 'Old Value',
4302              50 'New value' , 75 'changed by' , 90 ' changed on' .
4303
4304     WRITE : /5 sy-uline(95).
4305     LOOP AT ist_editpos .
4306
4307       WRITE :/5 wa_emddtl-docno(10), 20 ist_editpos-fname(15) ,
4308              35 ist_editpos-f_old(10) , 50 ist_editpos-f_new(10) ,
4309              75 ist_editpos-user(12) , 90 ist_editpos-date  .
4310
4311     ENDLOOP .
4312
4313     CLEAR ist_editpos .
4314     REFRESH ist_editpos .
4315     CLEAR ok_code .
4316   ENDFORM.                    " disp_chg_hstry

4526   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4527   *&      Form  check_reason_code_rfc
4528   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4529   *      Check reason code at the time of Refund/forfeit/Convert.
4530   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4531   *      -->P_ZMM_EMDREF_RSCODE  text
4532   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4533   FORM check_reason_code_rfc USING   p_rscode.
4534   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4535     SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_emdrscode
4536       WHERE rscode =  p_rscode.
4537     IF sy-subrc NE 0.
4538       MESSAGE e467(zmm).
4539     ENDIF.
4540   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4541     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
4542       IF p_rscode < 100 OR p_rscode > 199  .
4543         MESSAGE e464(zmm).
4544       ENDIF.
4545     ELSEIF  zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'EMD' .
4546       IF p_rscode < 200 OR p_rscode > 299  .
4547         MESSAGE e465(zmm).
4548       ENDIF.
4549     ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
4550       IF p_rscode < 300 OR p_rscode > 399  .
4551         MESSAGE e466(zmm).
4552       ENDIF.
4553   *-------  < Check Reason code > ---------------------------------------*
4554     ENDIF.
4555   ENDFORM.                    " check_reason_code_rfc
4556   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4557   *&      Form  CHECK_BGLC
4558   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4559   *       Check BG/LC Document
4560   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4561   *  -->  p1        text
4562   *  <--  p2        text
4563   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4564   FORM check_bglc.
4565     IF prev_okcode = 'BGLC' OR prev_okcode = 'AMEND' .
4566
4567       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-docno  IS INITIAL.
4568
4569         SELECT SINGLE * FROM zmm_emdhdr INTO wa_emdhdr
4570                    WHERE docno = zmm_emdhdr-docno.
4571         IF sy-subrc = 0.
4572
4573           PERFORM check_bglc_doc.
4574         ELSE.
4575           MESSAGE e411(zmm).
4576
4577         ENDIF.
4578       ENDIF.
4579     ELSE.
4580       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-docno  IS INITIAL.
4581         SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDHDR INTO WA_EMDHDR UP TO 1 ROWS
4582    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO
4583    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
4584    ENDSELECT.
4585         IF  sy-subrc = 0.
4586           IF prev_okcode = 'AMEND'.
4587             IF wa_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
4588               MESSAGE e460(zmm).
4589             ENDIF.
4590           ENDIF.
4591         ELSE.
4592           MESSAGE e411(zmm).
4593         ENDIF.
4594       ENDIF.
4595     ENDIF.
4596   ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_BGLC

4873         MODIFY zmm_emdhdr FROM wa_emdhdr . " Modify ZMM_EMDHDR ECC Table only after SRM TDPD Table Successful Updation.

4924   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4925   *&      Form  APPEND_ITAB_S160
4926   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4927   *      Append OKCODE to ist_tab
4928   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4929   *  -->  p1        text
4930   *  <--  p2        text
4931   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4932   FORM append_itab_s160.
4933
4934     REFRESH ist_tab .
4935     CLEAR ist_tab   .
4936     CLEAR wa_tab    .
4937
4938     IF ( prev_okcode = 'DISP' )  .
4939       MOVE 'SAVE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4940       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4941       MOVE 'DELE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4942       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4943       MOVE 'RESET'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4944       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4945     ELSEIF ( prev_okcode = 'DELE' )  .
4946       REFRESH ist_tab .
4947       CLEAR ist_tab   .
4948       CLEAR wa_tab    .
4949       MOVE 'SAVE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4950       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4951       MOVE 'RESET'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4952       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4953     ELSEIF ( prev_okcode = 'CHAN' OR prev_okcode = 'ECREA' )  .
4954       REFRESH ist_tab .
4955       CLEAR ist_tab   .
4956       CLEAR wa_tab    .
4957       MOVE 'DELE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4958       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4959       MOVE 'RESET'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4960       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4961     ELSEIF ( prev_okcode = 'REST' )  .
4962       REFRESH ist_tab .
4963       CLEAR ist_tab   .
4964       CLEAR wa_tab    .
4965       MOVE 'DELE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4966       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4967       MOVE 'SAVE'     TO  wa_tab-fcode .
4968       APPEND wa_tab   TO  ist_tab      .
4969     ENDIF.
4970
4971
4972   ENDFORM.                    " APPEND_ITAB_S160
4973   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4974   *&      Form  SAVE_CHANGES
4975   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
4976   *       Save Changes for SRM Screen 160.
4977   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4978   *  -->  p1        text
4979   *  <--  p2        text
4980   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
4981   FORM save_changes.
4982     MOVE-CORRESPONDING zmm_emdhdr TO wa_emdhdr .

4984     MOVE 'SRM'    TO  wa_emdhdr-trtyp .
4985     MOVE sy-uname TO wa_emdhdr-chby  .
4986     MOVE sy-datum TO wa_emdhdr-chon .
4987     MODIFY zmm_emdhdr FROM wa_emdhdr .
4988     IF sy-subrc = 0.
4989       MESSAGE i412(zmm) WITH g_idocno .
4990       PERFORM release_lock .
4991       PERFORM clear_screen_160 .
4992       LEAVE TO SCREEN '0100'.
4993     ELSE.
4994       MESSAGE e270(zmm).
4995     ENDIF.
4996   ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_CHANGES

5485              i_editpos TYPE TABLE OF cdshw WITH HEADER LINE,

5495             date LIKE sy-datum,

5502       EXPORTING

5509         no_position_found          = 1
5510         wrong_access_to_archive    = 2

5528             wrong_access_to_archive = 2

5535             ist_editpos-date = wa_header-udate .
5536             ist_editpos-time = wa_header-utime .
5537             APPEND ist_editpos .
5538           ENDLOOP.
5539         ENDIF.
5540       ENDLOOP.
5541     ENDIF.

5544             25 'Request Number' , 45 'changed by' , 60 ' Date' ,

5552               25 ist_editpos-f_new(16) ,

5555               75 ist_editpos-time(6).

5559   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5560   *&      Form  check_inst_group_etend
5561   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5562   *      Check Instrument Group Incase of e-Tenders
5563   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5564   *  -->  p1        text
5565   *  <--  p2        text
5566   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5567   FORM check_inst_group_etend.
5568   *+004
5569
5570     DATA: ist_emddtl_temp LIKE TABLE OF wa_emddtl.
5571   *&--> Check Whether Instrument Type entered is from same Group or not
5572   *&--> G1- DD,BC,CC G2-LC , G3-BG , G4-IP G5-IV G6-OP
5573
5574   **** check for Instrument type. This logic is laid for the reason
5575   ***** that no
5576   ***** (1) If Instrument type is DD then only BC and CC is permitted
5577   ***** (2) If Instrument is BG then only BG is permitted.
5578   ****  (3) IF Instrument is IV then only IV is permitted.
5579
5580     DATA: ist_instgrp TYPE TABLE OF zmm_emddtl-inst_type.
5581     DATA:  wa_instgrp TYPE zmm_emddtl-inst_type.
5582     DATA: l_inst(20).
5583     DATA: l_fd  .
5584     REFRESH ist_instgrp.
5585     LOOP AT ist_emddtl INTO wa_emddtl.
5586       wa_instgrp  = wa_emddtl-inst_type.
5587       APPEND wa_instgrp  TO ist_instgrp.
5588     ENDLOOP.
5589     SORT ist_instgrp .
5590     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ist_instgrp .
5591     LOOP AT ist_instgrp INTO wa_instgrp .
5592       CONCATENATE  l_inst
5593      wa_instgrp  INTO l_inst .
5594     ENDLOOP.
5595
5596     IF NOT l_inst IS INITIAL.
5597
5598       IF l_inst  = 'BCCCDD'  OR
5599          l_inst  = 'BCCC'    OR
5600          l_inst  = 'CCDD'    OR
5601          l_inst  = 'CCBC'    OR
5602          l_inst  = 'BCDD'    OR
5603          l_inst  = 'DD'      OR
5604          l_inst  = 'CC'      OR
5605          l_inst  = 'BC'      OR
5606          l_inst  = 'BG'      OR
5607          l_inst  = 'IP'      OR
5608          l_inst  = 'IV'      OR
5609          l_inst  = 'LC'     OR
5610          l_inst  = 'OP'.
5611         l_fd = 'X'.
5612         g_error = 1 .
5613       ELSE.
5614         g_error = 0.
5615         MESSAGE i426(zmm).
5616       ENDIF.
5617     ENDIF.
5618   ENDFORM.                    " check_inst_group_etend
5619   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5620   *&      Form  get_reqno
5621   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5622   *       Get Request no.
5623   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5624   *  -->  p1        text
5625   *  <--  p2        text
5626   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5627   FORM get_reqno.
5628
5629     DATA: wa_dtl TYPE zmm_emddtl.
5630     CLEAR: wa_dtl,g_reqno.
5631   * Start of change by SAB_SARVANAN 0n 12/06/2009
5632     SELECT * FROM ZMM_EMDDTL INTO WA_DTL UP TO 1 ROWS
5633    WHERE DOCNO = ZMM_EMDHDR-DOCNO AND RI_REQNO NE ' '
5634    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
5635    ENDSELECT.
5636   * End of change by SAB_SARVANAN 0n 12/06/2009
5637     IF sy-subrc = 0.
5638       IF NOT wa_dtl-ri_reqno IS INITIAL.
5639         IF  wa_dtl-ri_reqno+13(1) = 'R'.
5640           CONCATENATE g_idocno  wa_emddtl-item_no  'RC' INTO g_reqno .
5641         ELSEIF  wa_dtl-ri_reqno+13(1) = 'I'.
5642           CONCATENATE g_idocno  wa_emddtl-item_no  'IC' INTO g_reqno .
5643         ENDIF.
5644       ENDIF.
5645     ENDIF.
5646
5647   ENDFORM.                    " get_reqno

5658           l_text(15).

5660     DATA l_reqno  TYPE zmm_emddtl-ri_reqno.
5661     IF p_ad = 1.
5662       l_text = 'Return'.
5663       l_status = '1'.  "Request for Return
5664       CONCATENATE g_idocno  wa_emddtl-item_no  'RR' INTO g_reqno .
5665     ELSEIF p_ad = 2.
5666       l_text = 'Invoke'.
5667       l_status = '2'.  "Request for Invoke
5668       CONCATENATE g_idocno  wa_emddtl-item_no 'IR' INTO g_reqno .
5669     ENDIF.

5685   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5686   *&      Form  check_auth_RETURN
5687   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5688   *       Authority Check at the time of EMD/SD BG Return
5689   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5690   *      -->P_ZMM_EMDHDR_TRANS  text
5691   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5692   FORM check_auth_return USING    p_trans   .
5693     CLEAR : g_firet,g_mmret .
5694     DATA: wa_dtl TYPE zmm_emd_status.
5695     DATA: l_msg(30).
5696
5697     READ TABLE ist_emddtl INTO wa_dtl WITH KEY docno = zmm_emdhdr-docno
5698                                            sel = 'X'.
5699   * IF status = 'Accepted by Bank or Denied by Bank , then allow Retur
5700   * of BG of EMD Document. This Authorisation is with 'MM' under having
5701   *Activity '02'.
5702     IF ( wa_dtl-status = 'A'  OR wa_dtl-status = 'Y' ) AND
5703          wa_dtl-trans = 'EMD' .
5704       AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'ZMMBGLC'
5705              ID 'ACTVT' FIELD  '02'   .
5706       IF sy-subrc = 0 .
5707         g_mmret = 'X'.
5708       ENDIF.
5709
5710   *  ELSEIF wa_dtl-ri_stat = space OR wa_dtl-ri_stat = '3'  .
5711   *    MESSAGE i588(zmm).
5712   *    g_mmret = space .
5713   *    MESSAGE e505(zmm).
5714
5715     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status  = 'E'.
5716       g_ans = 1.
5717       g_error = 1.
5718       MESSAGE e452(zmm).
5719     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status = 'V' .
5720       MESSAGE e443(zmm).
5721       g_ans = 1.
5722       g_error = 1.
5723     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status = 'N'.
5724       g_ans = 1.
5725       g_error = 1.
5726       MESSAGE e497(zmm).
5727     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status = 'B'.
5728       g_ans = 1.
5729       g_error = 1.
5730       MESSAGE e447(zmm).
5731     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status = 'S' OR wa_dtl-status = 'P' OR
5732         wa_dtl-ri_stat = '1'.
5733       IF wa_dtl-status = 'S'.
5734         l_msg = 'Submitetd to FI'.
5735       ELSEIF  wa_dtl-status = 'P'.
5736         l_msg = 'Accepted by FI'.
5737       ELSEIF  wa_dtl-ri_stat = '1'.
5738         l_msg = 'Request for Return'.
5739       ENDIF.
5740
5741       AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'ZMMBGLC'
5742       ID 'ACTVT' FIELD  '16'   .
5743       IF sy-subrc = 0.
5744         g_firet = 'X'.
5745       ELSE.
5746         g_firet = space .
5747         g_ans = 1.
5748         g_error = 1.
5749         MESSAGE i513(zmm) WITH l_msg .
5750         MESSAGE e514(zmm) WITH l_msg .
5751       ENDIF.
5752
5753     ELSEIF wa_dtl-status = 'Y'  AND
5754            wa_dtl-trans = 'SDT'.
5755       AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'ZMMBGLC'
5756       ID 'ACTVT' FIELD  '02'   .
5757       IF sy-subrc = 0 .
5758         g_mmret = 'X'.
5759       ELSE.
5760         PERFORM check_fi_athority.
5761       ENDIF.
5762
5763     ELSE.
5764       g_mmret = space .
5765       MESSAGE e505(zmm).
5766     ENDIF.
5767
5768
5769   * If Status is "Submitted to FI" or "Accepted by FI"  or ri_status =
5770   *'1' or '2' then allow only FI person to Do Return.
5771   *
5772   * if wa_dtl-status = 'Y'  and
5773   *         wa_dtl-trans = 'SDT'.
5774   *    authority-check object 'ZMMBGLC'
5775   *    id 'ACTVT' field  '02'   .
5776   *    if sy-subrc = 0 .
5777   *      g_mmret = 'X'.
5778   *    else.
5779   *      perform check_fi_athority.
5780   *    endif.
5781   *endif.
5782
5783
5784     IF wa_dtl-ri_reqno+14(1) = 'R' AND wa_dtl-status = 'P'.
5785   *    authority-check object 'ZMMBGLC'
5786   *            id 'ACTVT' field  '16'   .
5787       IF sy-subrc NE 0 .
5788         MESSAGE e512(zmm).
5789       ELSE.
5790         g_firet = 'X'.
5791       ENDIF.
5792     ENDIF.
5793
5794
5795   *     if p_stat ne 'X'.
5796   *        if wa_emddtl-status = 'S'.
5797   *          g_ans = 1.
5798   *          g_error = 1.
5799   *          message i496(zmm).
5800   *        elseif wa_emddtl-status = 'A'.
5801   *          g_ans = 1.
5802   *          g_error = 1.
5803   *          message i450(zmm).
5804   *        endif.
5805
5806
5807   * if wa_dtl-inst_type = 'BG' and ( p_trans = 'EMD' or p_trans = 'SDT' )
5808   *.
5809   *    authority-check object 'ZMMBGLC'
5810   *           id 'ACTVT' field  '16'   .
5811   **
5812   *    if sy-subrc ne 0 .
5813   *      if wa_dtl-ri_stat ne '3' and wa_dtl-ri_reqno ne space.
5814   *        if not ( wa_dtl-status = 'A' or wa_dtl-status = 'Y' ) .
5815   *          message  e505(zmm).
5816   *          elseif ( wa_dtl-status = 'A' ).
5817   *             l_stat = space .
5818   *           else.
5819   *             l_stat = 'X'.
5820   *        endif.
5821   *      endif.
5822   *    endif.
5823   *  endif.
5824   ENDFORM.                    " check_auth_RETURN

5857         IF  wa_emddtl-status = 'E'.
5858           MESSAGE i452(zmm).
5859           g_ans = 1.
5860           g_error = 1.
5861           EXIT.
5862         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status = 'V' .
5863           MESSAGE i443(zmm).
5864           g_ans = 1.
5865           g_error = 1.
5866           EXIT.
5867         ELSEIF wa_emddtl-status NE 'P'.

5869           g_ans = 1.
5870           g_error = 1.
5871           EXIT.
5872         ELSE.
5873           g_ans = 0.
5874           g_error = 0.
5875         ENDIF.
5876       ENDLOOP.
5877     ENDIF.

5879   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

5882   *       text
5883   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5884   *  -->  p1        text
5885   *  <--  p2        text
5886   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5887   FORM send_to_srm .
5888     DATA l_logsys(32) TYPE c.
5889     IF zallow = 'Y'.
5890       SELECT SINGLE logsys FROM zmm_logsys INTO l_logsys
5891                 WHERE  appl = 'SRM'.
5892       IF NOT l_logsys IS INITIAL.
5893         CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
5894           EXPORTING
5895             input  = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno
5896           IMPORTING
5897             output = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno.
5898
5899         CALL FUNCTION 'Z_UPDATE_TF_ERP' DESTINATION l_logsys
5900           EXPORTING
5901             iv_bid_no             = zmm_emdhdr-ebidno               "'char10
5902             iv_changed_by         = sy-uname
5903             iv_vendor_no          = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno             "'char10
5904             iv_amount             = zmm_emdhdr-amount
5905             iv_currency           = zmm_emdhdr-currency
5906             iv_payment_date       = sy-datum
5907             iv_payment_time       = sy-uzeit
5908           IMPORTING
5909             ev_success            = lv_srm_update_status
5910           EXCEPTIONS
5911             tender_not_published  = 1
5912             tf_deadline_reached   = 2
5913   *         OTHERS                = 3.    "-009
5914             tender_fee_exempted   = 3     "+009
5915             tender_fee_paid       = 4     "+009
5916             bidder_not_registered = 5     "+009
5917             vendor_blocked        = 6     "+009
5918             OTHERS                = 7.    "+009
5919
5920         IF sy-subrc = 6.
5921           MESSAGE i011(zsrm).
5922         ENDIF.
5923
5924       ENDIF.
5925     ENDIF.
5926   ENDFORM.                    " SEND_TO_SRM

5934   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5935   FORM pass_to_srm .
5936     DATA l_logsys(32) TYPE c.
5937   * Checking the valid E-bid & Vendor
5938     IF zallow = 'Y' AND prev_okcode = 'ECREA' AND zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'TFS'.
5939   * Fetching the Logical system name from the table ZMM_LOGSYS
5940       SELECT SINGLE logsys FROM zmm_logsys INTO l_logsys
5941                 WHERE  appl = 'SRM'.
5942   * Checking value is initial
5943       IF NOT l_logsys IS INITIAL.

5945           EXPORTING
5946             input  = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno
5947           IMPORTING
5948             output = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno.
5949   * Function Module from SRM to pass the value
5950         CALL FUNCTION 'Z_UPDATE_TF_ERP' DESTINATION l_logsys
5951           EXPORTING
5952             iv_bid_no       = zmm_emdhdr-ebidno               "'char10
5953             iv_changed_by   = sy-uname
5954             iv_vendor_no    = zmm_emdhdr-vendorno             "'char10
5955             iv_amount       = zmm_emdhdr-amount
5956             iv_currency     = zmm_emdhdr-currency
5957             iv_payment_date = sy-datum
5958             iv_payment_time = sy-uzeit.
5959   * IMPORTING
5960   *   EV_SUCCESS                 =
5961   * EXCEPTIONS
5962   *   TENDER_NOT_PUBLISHED       = 1
5963   *   TF_DEADLINE_REACHED        = 2
5964   *   OTHERS                     = 3
5965       ENDIF.
5966     ENDIF.
5967   ENDFORM.                    " PASS_TO_SRM
5968
5969   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5970   *&      Module  CHECK_APRV_STATUS  INPUT
5971   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
5972   * To check SD/PBG approval status
5973   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
5974   MODULE check_aprv_status INPUT.
5975
5976     IF zmm_emdhdr-trans = 'SDT'.
5977
5978   *Future date validation : LOA
5979       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt IS INITIAL.
5980
5981         IF zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt GT sy-datum.
5982
5983           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-LOA_DT'.
5984           MESSAGE e981(zmm) WITH text-085.
5985
5986         ENDIF.
5987
5988       ENDIF.
5989
5990   * SD / PBG submission date > LOA date
5991       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt    IS INITIAL AND
5992          NOT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt IS INITIAL.
5993
5994         IF zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt LT zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt.
5995           MESSAGE e979(zmm).
5996         ENDIF.
5997
5998       ENDIF.
5999
6000   * Actual date of rcpt > LOA date
6001       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt    IS INITIAL AND
6002          NOT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt IS INITIAL.
6003
6004         IF zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt LT zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt.
6005           MESSAGE e980(zmm).
6006         ENDIF.
6007
6008       ENDIF.
6009
6010   *Future date validation : Actual date of receipt
6011       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt IS INITIAL.
6012
6013         IF zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt GT sy-datum.
6014
6015           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-SD_PBG_RCPT_DT'.
6016           MESSAGE e981(zmm) WITH text-086.
6017
6018         ENDIF.
6019
6020       ENDIF.
6021
6022   *+010 : Start
6023   * EPC Approval (Yes/No) ?
6024       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-apprv_chk IS INITIAL AND g_pmc = 'X'.
6025
6026         IF zmm_emdhdr-epc_chk IS INITIAL.
6027
6028           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-EPC_CHK'.
6029           MESSAGE e981(zmm) WITH text-089.
6030
6031         ENDIF.
6032
6033       ENDIF.
6034   *+010 : End
6035
6036   *Future date validation : Date of approval
6037       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-apprv_on IS INITIAL.
6038
6039         IF zmm_emdhdr-apprv_on GT sy-datum.
6040
6041           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_ON'.
6042           MESSAGE e981(zmm) WITH text-087.
6043
6044         ENDIF.
6045
6046       ENDIF.
6047
6048       IF prev_okcode = 'CHAN'.
6049         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-loa_no         TO wa_emdhdr-loa_no.
6050         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-loa_dt         TO wa_emdhdr-loa_dt.
6051         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt      TO wa_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt.
6052         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt TO wa_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt.
6053         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-apprv_chk      TO wa_emdhdr-apprv_chk.
6054         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-epc_chk        TO wa_emdhdr-epc_chk.     "+010
6055         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by       TO wa_emdhdr-apprv_by.
6056         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-apprv_on       TO wa_emdhdr-apprv_on.
6057         MOVE zmm_emdhdr-remarks        TO wa_emdhdr-remarks.
6058       ENDIF.
6059
6060       IF NOT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt      IS INITIAL AND
6061          NOT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt IS INITIAL.
6062
6063         IF zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt LT zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt.
6064
6065           PERFORM check_aprv_status USING g_pmc
6066                                           zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt
6067                                           zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt
6068                                           zmm_emdhdr-apprv_chk
6069                                           zmm_emdhdr-epc_chk          "+010
6070                                           zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by
6071                                           zmm_emdhdr-apprv_on
6072                                           zmm_emdhdr-remarks.
6073
6074         ELSEIF zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_dt GE zmm_emdhdr-sd_pbg_rcpt_dt AND
6075                zmm_emdhdr-apprv_chk = 'Y'.
6076
6077           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_CHK'.
6078           MESSAGE e011(zmm_emd).
6079
6080         ENDIF.
6081
6082       ENDIF.
6083
6084     ENDIF.
6085
6086   ENDMODULE.                 " CHECK_APRV_STATUS  INPUT
6087
6088   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6089   *&      Form  CHECK_APRV_STATUS
6090   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6091   * To validate SD/PBG approval details
6092   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6093   *      -->P_PMC        PMC Flag('X' -Do validation/ ' ' - No Validation)
6094   *      -->P_SD_PBG_DT  SD/PBG Submission deadline
6095   *      -->P_RCPT_DT    Actual date of Receipt of SD/PBG
6096   *      -->P_APPRV_CHK  Approved by competent authority Y / N
6097   *      -->P_EPC_CHK    EPC Approval Y / N
6098   *      -->P_APPRV_BY   Approver
6099   *      -->P_APPRV_ON   Approval date
6100   *      -->P_REMARKS    Reason for Delay
6101   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6102   FORM check_aprv_status  USING    p_pmc
6103                                    p_sd_pbg_dt
6104                                    p_rcpt_dt
6105                                    p_apprv_chk
6106                                    p_epc_chk
6107                                    p_apprv_by
6108                                    p_apprv_on
6109                                    p_remarks.
6110     DATA : l_days TYPE sy-tabix.
6111
6112     l_days = p_rcpt_dt - p_sd_pbg_dt.
6113
6114     IF l_days GT 0.
6115
6116       IF p_apprv_chk IS INITIAL.
6117
6118         SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_CHK'.
6119         MESSAGE e975(zmm).
6120
6121       ENDIF.
6122
6123       IF p_apprv_chk = 'Y' AND p_pmc = 'X'.
6124
6125   *Approver
6126         IF p_apprv_by IS INITIAL.
6127
6128           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_BY'.
6129           MESSAGE e976(zmm).
6130
6131         ELSE.
6132
6133           IF p_epc_chk = 'Y'.                      "+010
6134
6135             IF l_days LE 28.
6136               PERFORM chk_authority USING zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by
6137                                           'L1'.
6138             ELSE.
6139               PERFORM chk_authority USING zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by
6140                                           'DI'.
6141             ENDIF.
6142
6143   *+010 : Start
6144           ELSE.
6145
6146             PERFORM chk_authority USING zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by
6147                                         'L1'.
6148           ENDIF.
6149   *+010 : End
6150
6151         ENDIF.
6152
6153   *Approval date
6154         IF p_apprv_on IS INITIAL.
6155
6156           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_ON'.
6157           MESSAGE e977(zmm).
6158
6159         ENDIF.
6160   *Reason for Delay
6161         IF p_remarks IS INITIAL.
6162
6163           SET CURSOR FIELD 'ZMM_EMDHDR-REMARKS'.
6164           MESSAGE e978(zmm).
6165
6166         ENDIF.
6167
6168         zmm_emdhdr-status = 'N'.
6169
6170       ELSE.
6171
6172         zmm_emdhdr-status = '0'.
6173
6174       ENDIF.
6175
6176     ENDIF.

6240   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6241   *&      Form  GET_LOV_APPROVE_BY
6242   *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
6243   * Serach help on Approved by Competent Authority
6244   *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
6245   FORM get_lov_approve_by .
6246
6247     DATA : ist_return_tab      LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF ddshretval
6248                                            WITH  HEADER LINE.
6249
6250     DATA : ist_field_tab LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF dfies
6251                                              WITH  HEADER LINE.
6252     DATA : BEGIN OF wa_object,
6253              apprv_by TYPE agr_users-uname,
6254              ename    TYPE pa0001-ename,
6255            END OF wa_object.
6256
6257     DATA : ist_object_f4 LIKE STANDARD TABLE OF wa_object
6258                               INITIAL SIZE 100.
6259     DATA : BEGIN OF wa_users,
6260              uname TYPE pa0001-uname,
6261            END OF wa_users.
6262
6263     DATA : ist_users LIKE TABLE OF wa_users.
6264
6265     DATA : l_agr_name_1 TYPE agr_users-agr_name VALUE '%SRV%IND%1%',
6266            l_agr_name_2 TYPE agr_users-agr_name VALUE '%SRV%IND%DI'.
6267
6268     DATA : l_pernr  TYPE pa0001-pernr.
6269
6270     REFRESH : ist_object_f4,
6271               ist_return_tab,
6272               ist_users.
6273
6274     SELECT uname FROM agr_users INTO TABLE ist_users
6275             WHERE agr_name LIKE l_agr_name_1 OR
6276                   agr_name LIKE l_agr_name_2.
6277
6278     SORT ist_users BY uname.
6279
6280     DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM ist_users COMPARING uname.
6281
6282     LOOP AT ist_users INTO wa_users.
6283
6284       CLEAR wa_object.
6285
6286       MOVE wa_users-uname TO wa_object-apprv_by.
6287       MOVE wa_users-uname TO l_pernr.
6288
6289       SELECT ENAME FROM PA0001 INTO WA_OBJECT-ENAME UP TO 1 ROWS
6290    WHERE PERNR = L_PERNR AND ENDDA GE SY-DATUM
6291    ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY .
6292    ENDSELECT.
6293
6294       IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
6295         APPEND wa_object TO ist_object_f4.
6296       ENDIF.
6297
6298     ENDLOOP.
6299
6300     CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
6301       EXPORTING
6302         retfield        = 'APPRV_BY'
6303         dynpprog        = sy-repid
6304         dynpnr          = sy-dynnr
6305         dynprofield     = 'ZMM_EMDHDR-APPRV_BY'
6306         value_org       = 'S'
6307   *     callback_form   = 'F4CALLBACK'
6308       TABLES
6309         value_tab       = ist_object_f4
6310         field_tab       = ist_field_tab
6311         return_tab      = ist_return_tab
6312       EXCEPTIONS
6313         parameter_error = 1
6314         no_values_found = 2
6315         OTHERS          = 3.
6316     IF sy-subrc <> 0.
6317
6318       MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
6319               WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
6320
6321     ENDIF.
6322
6323     zmm_emdhdr-apprv_by =  ist_return_tab-fieldval.
6324
6325   ENDFORM.                    " GET_LOV_APPROVE_BY
